231: static void send_health(locker_core_t *core)
232: {
233:     if (core->device == NULL) {
234:         return;
235:     }
236:     locker_health_report_t report;
237:     memset(&report, 0, sizeof(report));
238:     report.host_instance_id = core->instance_id;
239:     /* UI 层在 S1-04 根本不存在：必须报「未知」，不能把 Core 自己的实例 ID 和
240:        进度计数器抄过去。以前 ui_instance_id=host_instance_id、
241:        ui_progress=core_progress、ui_age_ms=0，等于让 Core 活着就证明 UI 活着，
242:        监护侧据此会认为 UI 一直在新鲜推进（审计 R1-B-7，
243:        internal-interfaces.md:154「未知线上零 UUID / UINT32_MAX age」）。 */
244:     locker_uuid_clear(&report.ui_instance_id);
245:     report.core_progress = core->progress_counter;
246:     report.ui_progress = 0u;
247:     report.core_age_ms = 0u;
248:     report.ui_age_ms = UINT32_MAX;
249:     report.supervisor_state = LOCKER_SUPERVISOR_STATE_UNKNOWN;
250:     (void)locker_device_send_health(core->device, &report);
251: }
252: 
253: void domain_device_pump(locker_core_t *core)
254: {
255:     if (core->device == NULL) {
256:         return;
257:     }
258:     uint64_t now = domain_now(core);
259:     if (core->last_pump_ms != 0u && now - core->last_pump_ms < DOMAIN_POLL_INTERVAL_MS) {
260:         return;
261:     }
262:     core->last_pump_ms = now;
263: 
264:     /* 快照必须在会话门之前请求：device_session_up 是「快照已经成功过」的结果，
265:        拿它当快照的前置就是循环等待，真实链路上永远发不出第一次 GET_SNAPSHOT。
266:        设备端在 HELLO 完成前会返回 NOT_READY，这里清槽后下个泵周期重试。 */
267:     if (!core->snapshot_in_flight ||
268:         now - core->snapshot_requested_ms >= DOMAIN_SNAPSHOT_INTERVAL_MS) {
269:         request_snapshot(core);
270:     }
271: 
272:     if (!core->device_session_up) {
273:         return;
274:     }
275:     if (!core->fetch_in_flight || now - core->last_fetch_ms >= DOMAIN_FETCH_INTERVAL_MS) {
276:         request_fetch(core);
277:     }
278:     request_ack(core);
279:     if (core->last_health_ms == 0u || now - core->last_health_ms >= DOMAIN_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS) {
280:         core->last_health_ms = now;
281:         send_health(core);
282:     }
283: }
284: 
285: void domain_fetch_pump(locker_core_t *core)
286: {
287:     request_fetch(core);
288: }
289: 
290: /* ---------- 事件应用 ---------- */
291: 
292: static void wait_confirm_conflicted(locker_core_t *core);
293: 
294: static bool event_belongs_to_active(locker_core_t *core, const locker_device_event_t *event)
295: {
296:     if (!core->active.used) {
297:         return false;
298:     }
299:     if (locker_uuid_is_nil(&event->operation_id)) {
300:         return false;
301:     }
302:     return locker_uuid_equal(&event->operation_id, &core->active.id);
303: }
304: 
305: static void apply_physical_event(locker_core_t *core, const locker_device_event_t *event)
306: {
307:     domain_physical_t *physical = &core->physical;
308:     physical->valid = true;
309:     memcpy(physical->device_id, core->cursor.device_id, sizeof(physical->device_id));
310:     physical->journal_epoch = event->key.journal_epoch;
311:     physical->boot_id = event->key.boot_id;
312:     physical->physical_revision = event->physical_revision;
313:     physical->door = event->door;
314:     physical->item = event->item;
315:     if (event->supply_mv != 0xffffffffu) {
316:         physical->supply_valid = true;
317:         physical->supply_mv = event->supply_mv;
318:     }
319:     physical->observed_mono_ms = domain_now(core);
320:     physical->continuity_ok = core->cursor.continuity_ok;
321:     physical->dropped_events = 0u;
322:     physical->durable_cursor = event->key;
323:     core->device_snapshot_valid = true;
324:     /* 新的物理事实到达：等确认的依据立刻按它检查（R1-B-4）。
325:        快照路径同样要走这条规则，不能只在 DEVICE_EVENT 里查。 */
326:     wait_confirm_conflicted(core);
327: }
328: 
329: /* 目标变化：BORROW 期望物品离位，RETURN 期望物品在位。 */
330: static bool expected_item_for(const domain_operation_t *operation, uint8_t item)
331: {
332:     if (operation->kind == LOCKER_OPERATION_KIND_BORROW) {
333:         return item == LOCKER_ITEM_STATE_ABSENT;
334:     }
335:     if (operation->kind == LOCKER_OPERATION_KIND_RETURN) {
336:         return item == LOCKER_ITEM_STATE_PRESENT;
337:     }
338:     return false;
339: }
340: 
341: /*
342:  * WAIT_CONFIRM 期间的新物理事实必须作废确认依据。
343:  *
344:  * 进 WAIT_CONFIRM 时冻结了当时的 door/item/physical_revision。此后只要出现
345:  * 任何一种变化——门又开了、物品离开期望值、物理版本推进、物品变成 UNKNOWN——
346:  * 就说明「用户看到的柜内状态」已经和用户将要确认的那次动作无关了。
347:  * 以前 confirm 只比当前门/物品和那个旧的 item_transition_seen 布尔，
348:  * 「开一次门再关回去、物品放回原位」就能把冲突洗掉，用户照旧点确认完成
349:  * （审计 R1-B-4：探针实测开门后仍为 WAIT_CONFIRM，随后可正常 complete）。
350:  *
351:  * 依据：stage-00-architecture.md:118「每个确认只对应那一组物理证据」、
352:  * internal-interfaces.md:116。
353:  */
354: static void wait_confirm_conflicted(locker_core_t *core)
355: {
356:     domain_operation_t *operation = &core->active;
357:     if (!operation->used || operation->state != LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_WAIT_CONFIRM) {
358:         return;
359:     }
360:     bool conflicted = false;
361:     if (!operation->wait_confirm_basis_valid) {
362:         /* 恢复出来的操作没有冻结依据：按保守规则，任何新事实都不能被当作确认依据。 */
363:         conflicted = true;
364:     } else {
365:         if (core->physical.physical_revision != operation->wait_confirm_physical_revision) {
366:             conflicted = true;
367:         }
368:         if (core->physical.item == LOCKER_ITEM_STATE_UNKNOWN || core->physical.item != operation->wait_confirm_ite
m) {
369:             conflicted = true;
370:         }
371:         if (core->physical.door != operation->wait_confirm_door) {
372:             conflicted = true;
373:         }
374:     }
375:     if (!conflicted) {
376:         return;
377:     }
378:     (void)domain_require_review(core, operation, "PHYSICAL_CHANGED");
379: }
380: 
381: static void operation_advance_from_evidence(locker_core_t *core, domain_operation_t *operation)
382: {
383:     if (operation->state != LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_ACTIVE) {
384:         return;
385:     }
386:     if (!operation->door_open_seen || core->physical.door != LOCKER_DOOR_STATE_CLOSED) {
387:         return;
388:     }
389:     bool changed = expected_item_for(operation, core->physical.item);
390:     uint8_t target = changed ? (uint8_t)LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_WAIT_CONFIRM
391:                              : (uint8_t)LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_NO_CHANGE;
392:     const char *reason = changed ? "PHYSICAL_CONFIRMED" : "NO_ITEM_CHANGE";
393: 
394:     /* 状态跃迁、物理快照、占位释放在同一个候选副本 + 同一个 change 里完成：
395:        NO_CHANGE 是终态，占位必须和终态一起落库。只清内存 used 不删
396:        slot_guards，下一次申请的 ABSENT 检查会永远冲突，重启后也修不回来
397:        （审计 R1-B-2：探针实测 state=NO_CHANGE、active=0、guard_remains=1）。
398:        提交失败则内存保持 ACCESS_ACTIVE，下一帧证据还会再试一次。 */
399:     domain_operation_t candidate = *operation;
400:     if (domain_operation_set_state(core, &candidate, target, reason) != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
401:         return;
402:     }
403:     domain_operation_capture_physical(&candidate, &core->physical);
404:     domain_change_t change;
405:     domain_change_init(core, &change, core->revision, core->revision + 1u);
406:     if (domain_operation_persist(core, &change, &candidate) != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
407:         return;
408:     }
409:     if (!changed && !domain_change_delete(&change, "slot_guards", "1")) {
410:         return;
411:     }
412:     if (domain_change_commit(core, &change) != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
413:         return;
414:     }
415:     /* NO_CHANGE 是终态：柜格占位（内存 used 与仓库 slot_guards）都释放，
416:        但状态与原因要留在这里——上层要靠它解释「开了门却什么都没拿走」，
417:        整个 memset 掉会让操作看起来从未发生过（测试与 5xx 说明都读 state）。 */
418:     if (!changed) {
419:         candidate.used = false;
420:     }
421:     *operation = candidate;
422:     if (changed && operation == &core->active) {
423:         /* 进 WAIT_CONFIRM 时冻结物理依据：等待用户确认期间任何新的物理事实
424:            都必须让这份依据作废并立刻进核验（见 wait_confirm_conflicted）。 */
425:         core->active.wait_confirm_basis_valid = true;
426:         core->active.wait_confirm_physical_revision = core->physical.physical_revision;
427:         core->active.wait_confirm_item = core->physical.item;
428:         core->active.wait_confirm_door = core->physical.door;
429:     }
430:     domain_publish(core, &candidate.id, "operation", core->revision);
431: }
432: 
433: void domain_event_apply(locker_core_t *core, const locker_device_event_t *event)
434: {
435:     if (core == NULL || event == NULL) {
436:         return;
437:     }
438:     /* 只接受当前日志的事件；跨 epoch 的历史补传只走仓储导入，不改内存状态。 */
439:     if (core->cursor.valid && !locker_uuid_equal(&core->cursor.journal_epoch, &event->key.journal_epoch)) {
440:         return;
441:     }
442:     if (core->cursor.valid && key_compare(&event->key, &core->cursor.through) <= 0) {
443:         return; /* 已应用过 */
444:     }
445:     core->cursor.valid = true;
446:     core->cursor.journal_epoch = event->key.journal_epoch;
447:     core->cursor.through = event->key;
448:     /* live 事件带 24 位 device_id（适配器按握手身份填写）：用它补齐身份，
449:        补取页面才能构造出合法的 EventKey（wire 游标不含 device_id）。 */
450:     domain_learn_device_id(core, event->key.device_id);
451:     if (!event->live_transport) {
452:         core->cursor.continuity_ok = false;
453:         core->fetch_gap = true;
454:     }
455: 
456:     apply_physical_event(core, event);
457: 
458:     switch (event->event_type) {
459:     case LOCKER_DEVICE_EVENT_DOOR_OPENED:
460:         if (core->active.used) {
461:             core->active.door_open_seen = true;
462:             if (core->active.state == LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_ACTIVE &&
463:                 core->active.door_opened_ms == 0u) {
464:                 core->active.door_opened_ms = domain_now(core);
465:             }
466:         }
467:         break;
468:     case LOCKER_DEVICE_EVENT_ITEM_REMOVED:
469:     case LOCKER_DEVICE_EVENT_ITEM_PRESENT:
470:         if (core->active.used) {
471:             core->active.item_transition_seen = true;
472:         }
473:         break;
474:     case LOCKER_DEVICE_EVENT_PULSE_STARTED:
475:         if (core->active.used &&
476:             core->active.state == LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_PENDING) {
477:             if (domain_operation_set_state(core, &core->active, LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_ACTIVE,
478:                                            "PULSE_STARTED") == LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
479:                 core->active.pulse_started_ms = domain_now(core);
480:                 (void)domain_persist_operation(core, &core->active);
481:                 domain_publish(core, &core->active.id, "operation", core->revision);
482:             }
483:         }
484:         break;
485:     case LOCKER_DEVICE_EVENT_CONTINUITY_LOST:
486:     case LOCKER_DEVICE_EVENT_JOURNAL_FAULT:
487:         core->cursor.continuity_ok = false;
488:         core->fetch_gap = true;
489:         break;
490:     default:
491:         break;
492:     }
493: 
494:     if (core->active.used && event_belongs_to_active(core, event)) {
495:         domain_operation_add_source_event(&core->active, &event->key);
496:         if (!event->live_transport) {
497:             core->active.history_complete = false;
498:         }
499:     }
500:     /* 等确认期间的新物理事实立刻作废确认依据（R1-B-4）。
501:        放在 advance 之前：冲突优先于「按旧证据推进」。 */
502:     wait_confirm_conflicted(core);
503:     operation_advance_from_evidence(core, &core->active);
504: }
505: 
506: /* ---------- 响应应用 ---------- */
507: 
508: static void apply_snapshot(locker_core_t *core, const locker_wire_get_snapshot_response_t *snapshot)
509: {
510:     if (snapshot->result != 0u) {
511:         core->snapshot_in_flight = false;
512:         /* 快照取不到（设备忙/日志不可用/链路拒绝）就不再持有「已知物理事实」：
513:            保留 device_snapshot_valid=true 会让缓存值无限期有效，管理员或超时
514:            推进都能拿着几小时前的 door/item 去改账（审计 R1-B-12）。
515:            物理事实本身保留在 physical 里供诊断，但投影与准入改用 valid=false。 */
516:         core->device_snapshot_valid = false;
517:         core->physical.valid = false;
518:         return;
519:     }
520:     core->device_session_up = true;
521:     core->session.valid = true;
522:     core->session.session_id = snapshot->session_id;
523:     core->session.journal_epoch = snapshot->journal_epoch;
524:     core->session.boot_id = snapshot->boot_id;
525:     core->session.device_mode = session_mode_from_wire(snapshot->device_mode);
526:     core->session.journal_state = snapshot->journal_state;
527:     core->session.action_watermark = snapshot->action_watermark;
528:     core->action_watermark = snapshot->action_watermark;
529: 
530:     if (!core->cursor.valid) {
531:         core->cursor.valid = true;
532:         core->cursor.journal_epoch = snapshot->journal_epoch;
533:         /* through 是完整 EventKey：补取请求把它原样当 after 送设备端口，
534:            落库时又被 PhysicalSnapshot.durable_cursor 引用（两项都绕不过去）。
535:            两个约束必须同时满足：
536:            1) uart-v1.md:38 —— cursor 的 (0,0) 是「开始/空日志」哨兵，
537:               禁止只有 boot_id/event_seq 之一为 0。所以水位必须留在 (0,0)，
538:               不能把快照的 boot_id 配 0 号 event_seq（半零游标会被
539:               port.c:device_cursor_matches 第一行直接判非法 → CONFLICT →
540:               补取永不发出 → import_cursor 不推进 → ACK 不产生 →
541:               设备停在 RECONCILING）。
542:            2) internal-data.schema.json 的 EventKey 要求 device_id 匹配
543:               ^[0-9a-f]{24}$ 且 journal_epoch 是合法 UUID；空 device_id 会让
544:               device_state 文档校验失败。
545:            因此这里只补身份与 epoch，boot/seq 保持 0；水位随后由
546:            domain_import_page 用仓储提交值推进。设备身份来自更早的 HELLO 回复
547:            （domain_reply_apply case 1 → domain_learn_device_id）。 */
548:         memset(&core->cursor.through, 0, sizeof(core->cursor.through));
549:         memcpy(core->cursor.through.device_id, core->cursor.device_id,
550:                sizeof(core->cursor.through.device_id));
551:         core->cursor.through.journal_epoch = snapshot->journal_epoch;
552:     }
553: 
554:     domain_physical_t *physical = &core->physical;
555:     physical->valid = true;
556:     /* 设备身份只能来自 HELLO（domain_learn_device_uid）：GET_SNAPSHOT 回复本身
557:        不带 device_uid。这里必须把已知身份写进 physical，否则
558:        domain_persist_device_state 会因 device_id 不是 24 位十六进制直接
559:        NOT_READY，device_state 集合永远为空、重启后拿不到上次的物理基线。 */
560:     memcpy(physical->device_id, core->cursor.device_id, sizeof(physical->device_id));
561:     physical->journal_epoch = snapshot->journal_epoch;
562:     physical->boot_id = snapshot->boot_id;
563:     physical->physical_revision = snapshot->physical_revision;
564:     physical->door = snapshot->door;
565:     physical->item = snapshot->item;
566:     if ((snapshot->flags & 0x0001u) != 0u) {
567:         physical->supply_valid = true;
568:         physical->supply_mv = snapshot->supply_mv;
569:     }
570:     physical->observed_mono_ms = domain_now(core);
571:     physical->time_quality = LOCKER_TIME_QUALITY_HOST_SYNCED;
572:     physical->continuity_ok = core->cursor.continuity_ok;
573:     physical->dropped_events = snapshot->dropped_events;
574:     physical->durable_cursor = core->cursor.through;
575:     if ((snapshot->flags & 0x0004u) != 0u) {
576:         physical->continuity_ok = false;
577:         core->cursor.continuity_ok = false;
578:     }
579:     core->device_snapshot_valid = true;
580:     core->snapshot_in_flight = false;
581: }

623: static void apply_execute(locker_core_t *core, const locker_wire_execute_unlock_response_t *response)
624: {
625:     core->execute_in_flight = false;

627: 
628:     /* 失败应答没有 body：codec 把非零 result 折叠成 18 字节前缀
629:        （「A failed response is exactly the 18-byte prefix and nothing else」），
630:        command_id / operation_id 全零，拿它们做匹配必然失败——EXECUTE 已发出、
631:        应答已回来，操作却永远停在 ACCESS_PENDING 等到 OPEN_TIMEOUT
632:        （集成测试五个注入阶段实测卡死）。EXECUTE 同一时刻最多一条在飞
633:        （outbox_pump 的 execute_in_flight 门），所以失败应答直接落到当前
634:        挂起的命令上；成功应答仍然要求 body 与 pending 一致。 */
635:     if (response->result != LOCKER_WIRE_RESULT_OK &&

638:         /* 只在「已武装、等执行应答」时才落状态：失败应答不会启动脉冲，
639:            若状态已经前进（事件流已推进），这条失败只能是迟到的旧应答。 */
640:         if (!operation->used || operation->state != LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_PENDING) {
641:             return;
642:         }

649:             /* 设备侧明确拒绝：结果已定，清 pending 停掉查询补救。 */
650:             core->pending_command_valid = false;
651:             (void)domain_operation_set_state(core, operation, LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REVIEW_REQUIRED,
652:                                              "COMMAND_REJECTED");
653:             break;
654:         case LOCKER_WIRE_RESULT_UNKNOWN_RESULT:
655:         case LOCKER_WIRE_RESULT_LOG_UNAVAILABLE:
656:             /* 结果未知：设备自己都无法裁决，同样停止查询（查也查不回）。 */
657:             core->pending_command_valid = false;
658:             (void)domain_operation_set_state(core, operation, LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REVIEW_REQUIRED,
659:                                              "UNKNOWN_RESULT");
660:             break;
661:         default:
662:             /* 会话/传输层失败码（NOT_READY/BUSY 等）：命令仍可能已被受理，
663:                保留 pending 让 domain_timeouts 的「只查询不重发」补救继续工作。 */
664:             return;
665:         }

667:         domain_publish(core, &operation->id, "operation", core->revision);
668:         return;

674:     core->pending_command_valid = false;
675:     if (!operation->used || locker_uuid_is_nil(&response->operation_id) ||

712: static void apply_query_command(locker_core_t *core, const locker_wire_query_command_response_t *response)
713: {
714:     domain_operation_t *operation = &core->active;
715:     if (!operation->used) {
716:         return;
717:     }
718:     /* 查询是对一条真实命令的核对，应答总是回显完整 body；
719:        但若设备/链路层折叠出 body 全零的失败应答（同 apply_execute 的
720:        18 字节前缀形态），ID 门会把它拦成静默丢弃——查询本来就是为了
721:        解开「结果未知」，此时应按失败映射进复核，而不是卡住状态机。 */
722:     const bool body_nil = locker_uuid_is_nil(&response->operation_id);
723:     if (!body_nil) {
724:         if (!locker_uuid_equal(&response->operation_id, &operation->id)) {
725:             return;
726:         }
727:     }
728:     /* 查询就是「在飞 EXECUTE 的收尾手段」：应答一回来就说明主机不再等它了。
729:        以前只在 apply_execute / reset 清这个标志，丢应答的路径上
730:        execute_in_flight 永久为真：outbox_pump 的发送门永远关闭，后续任何
731:        合法新命令都发不出去；「放弃等待」又要求 !execute_in_flight 才清
732:        pending，于是整台柜子卡死在一条旧命令上（审计 R1-B-11）。 */
733:     if (core->pending_command_valid && !locker_uuid_is_nil(&core->pending_command_id)) {
734:         core->execute_in_flight = false;
735:     }
736:     if (response->result != LOCKER_WIRE_RESULT_OK) {
737:         /* 查询给出了确定结论：无论能否对应到 operation，都停止继续查询。 */
738:         core->pending_command_valid = false;
739:         if (!body_nil) {
740:             if (domain_operation_set_state(core, operation, LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REVIEW_REQUIRED,
741:                                            "QUERY_UNRESOLVED") == LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
742:                 (void)domain_persist_operation(core, operation);
743:                 domain_publish(core, &operation->id, "operation", core->revision);
744:             }
745:         }
746:         return;
747:     }
748:     core->pending_command_valid = false;
749:     if (response->command_state == LOCKER_COMMAND_STATE_PULSE_STARTED &&
750:         operation->state == LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_PENDING) {
751:         if (domain_operation_set_state(core, operation, LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_ACTIVE,
752:                                        "PULSE_STARTED") == LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
753:             operation->pulse_started_ms = domain_now(core);
754:             (void)domain_persist_operation(core, operation);
755:             domain_publish(core, &operation->id, "operation", core->revision);
756:         }
757:     }
758: }

760: void domain_reply_apply(locker_core_t *core, const locker_device_reply_t *reply)
761: {
762:     if (core == NULL || reply == NULL) {
763:         return;
764:     }
765:     uint8_t type = (uint8_t)(reply->message_type & 0x7fu);
766:     uint8_t purpose = DOMAIN_PURPOSE_NONE;
767:     (void)domain_pending_take(core, reply->request_no, &purpose);
768: 
769:     switch (type) {
770:     case 2u:
771:         apply_snapshot(core, &reply->body.get_snapshot);
772:         break;
773:     case 3u:
774:         apply_fetch(core, &reply->body.fetch_events);
775:         break;
776:     case 4u:
777:         apply_ack(core, &reply->body.ack_persisted);
778:         break;
779:     case 7u:
780:         apply_execute(core, &reply->body.execute_unlock);
781:         break;
782:     case 8u:
783:         apply_query_command(core, &reply->body.query_command);
784:         break;
785:     case 1u:
786:         /* HELLO 是设备身份的唯一权威来源（12 字节 UID -> 24 位小写 hex）。
787:            适配器已核对过 expected_device_id 与必要能力位，Core 只记录身份。 */
788:         if (reply->body.hello.result == 0u) {
789:             domain_learn_device_uid(core, reply->body.hello.device_uid);
790:             core->session.device_mode = session_mode_from_wire(reply->body.hello.device_mode);
791:             core->session.journal_state = reply->body.hello.journal_state;
792:         }
793:         break;
794:     case 5u:
795:         /* 失败应答被折叠成 18 字节前缀（body 全零）：device_mode=0 会被
796:            解释成 OFFLINE 并把已经 READY 的会话拉下马，之后再也没人恢复
797:            （只有 HELLO/RESUME_READY 能再写 mode）。只有 result==OK 的
798:            应答才允许改会话状态——失败的 RESUME_READY 只能说明「设备还
799:            没准备好放行」，不能反向证明会话失效。 */
800:         if (reply->body.resume_ready.result == LOCKER_WIRE_RESULT_OK) {
801:             core->session.valid = true;
802:             core->session.device_mode = session_mode_from_wire(reply->body.resume_ready.device_mode);
803:         }
804:         break;
805:     case 9u:
806:         /* 同 case 5：host_health 的失败应答（NOT_READY 等）body 全零，
807:            无条件写 mode 会把 READY 会话误降级（集成测试 borrow 流程实测
808:            CONFIRM_PRECONDITION 由这里引起）。 */
809:         if (reply->body.host_health.result == LOCKER_WIRE_RESULT_OK) {
810:             core->session.device_mode = session_mode_from_wire(reply->body.host_health.device_mode);
811:         }
812:         break;
813:     case 11u:
814:         if (reply->body.release_context.result == LOCKER_WIRE_RESULT_OK) {
815:             core->physical.physical_revision = reply->body.release_context.physical_revision;
816:             /* 设备侧已经放掉动作上下文：下一次 EXECUTE 不会再撞 has_active。
817:                释放本身不改连续性、不动水位、不重开旧动作（uart-v1.md:402）。 */
818:             core->release_pending = false;
819:             core->release_sent = false;
820:         }
821:         break;
822:     case 10u:
823:         /* TIME_ANCHOR：mcu 与主机单调时钟的保守偏移区间。没有这个分支，
824:            主机时间会被直接当成 MCU 时间（审计 R1-B-6）。 */
825:         domain_anchor_apply(core, reply);
826:         break;
827:     default:
828:         break;
829:     }
830: }

834: locker_status_t domain_execute_intent(locker_core_t *core, domain_operation_t *operation)
835: {
836:     if (core->device == NULL || !core->device_session_up || !core->session.valid) {
837:         return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
838:     }
839:     if (core->execute_in_flight) {
840:         return LOCKER_STATUS_BUSY;
841:     }
842:     locker_unlock_intent_t intent;
843:     memset(&intent, 0, sizeof(intent));
844:     intent.operation_id = operation->id;
845:     intent.command_id = operation->command_id;
846:     intent.session_id = core->session.session_id;
847:     intent.journal_epoch = core->session.journal_epoch;
848:     intent.expected_boot_id = core->session.boot_id;
849:     intent.expected_physical_revision = core->physical.physical_revision;
850:     intent.action_no = operation->action_no;
851:     intent.not_after_mcu_ms = core->now_ms + LOCKER_LOCK_PULSE_MAX_MS;
852:     intent.slot_id = 1u;
853:     intent.kind = (locker_operation_kind_t)operation->kind;
854:     locker_status_t status = locker_device_execute_once(core->device, &intent);
855:     if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
856:         return status;
857:     }
858:     core->execute_in_flight = true;
859:     core->pending_command_id = operation->command_id;
860:     core->pending_command_valid = true;
861:     core->pending_command_ms = domain_now(core);
862:     return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
863: }
864: 
865: locker_status_t domain_query_command(locker_core_t *core, const locker_uuid_t *command_id)
866: {
867:     if (core->device == NULL || !core->device_session_up) {
868:         return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
869:     }
870:     return locker_device_query_command(core->device, command_id);
871: }

158:                parse 出来的 updated_ms 是 Unix epoch 毫秒（量级 1.7e12），
159:                而 core->now_ms 是进程单调毫秒（量级 1e6）。直接把两者相加
160:                会得到 1.7e12 量级的 deadline，永远大于 now —— 重启前挂着的
161:                申请/待确认操作就再也不会超时，占位一直不释放（审计 R1-B-5：
162:                探针实测 mono=1000、wait_confirm_deadline_ms=1761004920000）。
163:                正确做法：先按同一时刻的墙钟算出「还剩多久」，再把剩余时长
164:                折算到当前单调时间。墙钟不可用或已经过期时，剩余时长按 0 处理
165:                （保守：立即到期，交给超时路径重新核验），不能当作「刚创建」。 */
166:             uint64_t now_wall = 0u;
167:             const bool wall_ok = locker_clock_port_wall_utc_ms(&core->clock, &now_wall) == LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
168:             const uint64_t now_mono = domain_now(core);
169:             if (loaded.state == LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REQUESTED) {
170:                 core->active.requested_deadline_ms =
171:                     deadline_in_monotonic(loaded.updated_ms, LOCKER_REQUEST_TTL_MS, wall_ok, now_wall, now_mono);
172:             } else if (loaded.state == LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_WAIT_CONFIRM) {
173:                 core->active.wait_confirm_deadline_ms =
174:                     deadline_in_monotonic(loaded.updated_ms, LOCKER_WAIT_CONFIRM_MS, wall_ok, now_wall, now_mono);
175:             }
176:         }

179:         }

185: /*
186:  * 把一个「墙钟 UTC 起点 + 时长」的期限换算到单调时间域。
187:  * 完整说明见文件上方的前置声明处。
188:  */
189: static uint64_t deadline_in_monotonic(uint64_t wall_start_ms, uint64_t duration_ms, bool wall_ok,
190:                                       uint64_t now_wall_ms, uint64_t now_mono_ms)
191: {
192:     if (!wall_ok || wall_start_ms == 0u) {
193:         return now_mono_ms;
194:     }
195:     if (now_wall_ms >= wall_start_ms) {
196:         uint64_t elapsed = now_wall_ms - wall_start_ms;
197:         if (elapsed >= duration_ms) {
198:             return now_mono_ms; /* 已过期：立即到期 */
199:         }
200:         return now_mono_ms + (duration_ms - elapsed);
201:     }
202:     return now_mono_ms + duration_ms;
203: }

409: locker_status_t domain_recover(locker_core_t *core)
410: {    if (core == NULL || core->repo == NULL) {
411:         return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
412:     }
413:     core->phase = DOMAIN_PHASE_RECOVERING;
414:     locker_status_t status = domain_repo_refresh_revision(core);
415:     if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
416:         return status;
417:     }
418:     status = recover_operations(core);
419:     if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
420:         return status;
421:     }
422:     status = recover_loans(core);
423:     if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
424:         return status;
425:     }
426:     /* 工具投影必须在恢复阶段完成：create 要靠它校验 tool_id/tool_revision，
427:        TOOL_GET 要靠它出内容（审计 R1-B-9）。 */
428:     status = recover_tools(core);
429:     if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
430:         return status;
431:     }
432:     status = recover_import_cursor(core);
433:     if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
434:         return status;
435:     }
436:     status = recover_device_state(core);
437:     if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
438:         return status;
439:     }
440:     /* 动作号必须抬到已见水位之上：sim 要求 action_no == watermark + 1，
441:        重启后复用旧号会被 REPLAY_BLOCKED。 */
442:     core->next_action_no = core->action_watermark + 1u;
443:     if (core->next_action_no == 0u) {
444:         core->next_action_no = 1u;
445:     }
446:     /* 恢复出来的可能已发送意图进核验，不恢复发送队列（internal-interfaces.md:114）。 */
447:     if (core->active.used && (core->active.state == LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_PENDING ||
448:                               core->active.state == LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_ACTIVE)) {
449:         if (domain_operation_set_state(core, &core->active, LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REVIEW_REQUIRED,
450:                                        "RECOVERED_AFTER_RESTART") == LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
451:             (void)domain_persist_operation(core, &core->active);
452:         }
453:     }
454:     core->recovery_ready = true;
455:     core->recovery_done = true;
456:     return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
457: }

494: static void core_run_once(locker_core_t *core)
#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L

/*
 * S1-04 业务核心：主机 <-> MCU 单调时钟换算。
 *
 * docs/architecture/internal-interfaces.md:133 的规则：设主机发起时刻 h0、
 * 收到响应的时刻 h3、MCU 收到请求的单调时刻 m1、MCU 发出的 m2，则真实偏移
 * 一定落在 [m2 - h3, m1 - h0] 这个保守区间里。只有 RTT <= 200 ms、样本年龄
 * <= 3000 ms 且 boot 一致时才允许用它换算动作期限；否则一律 fail closed，
 * 不猜、不外推（EXECUTE 的 not_after_mcu_ms 直接不发）。
 *
 * 本文件不做任何阻塞 IO：request 只是登记一个在途请求并调用适配器。
 */

#include "domain_internal.h"

#include <string.h>

/* 保守区间：把往返误差整体吃进两侧边界，宁可窄一点也不要放宽期限。 */
static void anchor_apply_sample(locker_core_t *core, uint64_t h0, uint64_t h3, uint64_t m1, uint64_t m2)
{
    int64_t low = (int64_t)m2 - (int64_t)h3;
    int64_t high = (int64_t)m1 - (int64_t)h0;
    if (low > high) {
        /* 乱序样本（MCU 时钟被拉回或主机时钟回退）：不采纳。 */
        core->anchor_valid = false;
        return;
    }
    core->anchor_offset_low_ms = low;
    core->anchor_offset_high_ms = high;
    core->anchor_sampled_ms = h3;
    core->anchor_valid = true;
}

locker_status_t domain_anchor_request(locker_core_t *core)
{
    if (core == NULL || core->device == NULL || !core->device_session_up || !core->session.valid) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    uint64_t now = domain_now(core);
    /* 采集要按周期做，不是每轮循环都发：Core 的 tick 是百毫秒级，逐轮回请
       会把 UART 带宽吃光，也让在途槽一直占着（审计 R1-B-6 的「按规定周期
       请求而非每轮请求」）。只有样本缺失或已过期才提前重采。 */
    if (now >= core->last_anchor_ms && now - core->last_anchor_ms < LOCKER_TIME_ANCHOR_PERIOD_MS) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_BUSY;
    }
    /* 同一时刻只允许一个在途锚：多余请求只会浪费 UART 带宽。 */
    for (uint32_t index = 0u; index < LOCKER_DOMAIN_PENDING_SLOTS; index += 1u) {
        if (core->pending[index].used && core->pending[index].purpose == DOMAIN_PURPOSE_ANCHOR) {
            return LOCKER_STATUS_BUSY;
        }
    }
    uint32_t request_no = domain_request_no(core, DOMAIN_PURPOSE_ANCHOR);
    if (request_no == 0u) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_BUSY;
    }
    core->last_anchor_ms = now;
    locker_status_t status = locker_device_time_anchor(core->device, request_no);
    if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        domain_pending_clear(core, DOMAIN_PURPOSE_ANCHOR);
    }
    return status;
}

void domain_anchor_apply(locker_core_t *core, const locker_device_reply_t *reply)
{
    if (core == NULL || reply == NULL) {
        return;
    }
    const locker_wire_time_anchor_response_t *anchor = &reply->body.time_anchor;
    if (anchor->result != LOCKER_WIRE_RESULT_OK) {
        core->anchor_valid = false;
        return;
    }
    /* boot 不一致说明中间重启过：旧样本不能跨 boot 使用。 */
    if (!core->session.valid || anchor->boot_id != core->session.boot_id) {
        core->anchor_valid = false;
        return;
    }
    uint64_t h0 = reply->request_started_host_mono_ms;
    uint64_t h3 = reply->received_host_mono_ms;
    if (h3 < h0) {
        core->anchor_valid = false;
        return;
    }
    uint64_t rtt = h3 - h0;
    if (rtt > LOCKER_TIME_ANCHOR_MAX_RTT_MS) {
        core->anchor_valid = false;
        return;
    }
    if (anchor->mcu_send_mono_ms < anchor->mcu_receive_mono_ms) {
        core->anchor_valid = false;
        return;
    }
    anchor_apply_sample(core, h0, h3, anchor->mcu_receive_mono_ms, anchor->mcu_send_mono_ms);
}

locker_status_t domain_anchor_mcu_now(const locker_core_t *core, uint64_t *out_low, uint64_t *out_high)
{
    if (core == NULL || out_low == NULL || out_high == NULL) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    if (!core->anchor_valid) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    uint64_t now = domain_now((locker_core_t *)core);
    if (now < core->anchor_sampled_ms) {
        /* 单调时钟回退：样本作废。 */
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    uint64_t age = now - core->anchor_sampled_ms;
    if (age > LOCKER_TIME_ANCHOR_MAX_AGE_MS) {
        /* 样本太旧：换算误差已经不可界定，宁可拒绝动作。 */
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    int64_t base = (int64_t)now;
    int64_t low = base + core->anchor_offset_low_ms;
    int64_t high = base + core->anchor_offset_high_ms;
    if (low < 0 || high < 0) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    *out_low = (uint64_t)low;
    *out_high = (uint64_t)high;
    return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
}

#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L

/* S1-04 业务核心测试支撑：假仓储（4 个端口）、假设备（10 个端口）、假发布器、
   可注入测试时钟，以及把 Core 直接推到「READY 且设备已就绪」的夹具。

   这些替身只存在于测试二进制里：host/domain 的公共 API 不暴露任何 setter，
   生产代码拿不到它们的入口（host/common/include/locker/common/clock.h:9-12）。 */

#ifndef DOMAIN_TEST_SUPPORT_H
#define DOMAIN_TEST_SUPPORT_H

#include "domain_internal.h"
#include "test_support.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#if defined(__GNUC__)
#define LOCKER_TEST_HELPER __attribute__((unused))
#else
#define LOCKER_TEST_HELPER
#endif

#define DT_MAX_DOCS 64u
#define DT_VALUE_BYTES 8192u
#define DT_POOL_NODES 2048u
#define DT_POOL_MEMBERS 4096u
#define DT_DEVICE_ID "0123456789abcdef01234567"
#define DT_TOKEN_HASH "aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa"

/* ---------- 可注入测试时钟 ---------- */

typedef struct test_clock {
    uint64_t monotonic_ms;
    uint64_t wall_ms;
    uint32_t uuid_counter;
} test_clock_t;

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static uint64_t dt_clock_monotonic(void *user)
{
    test_clock_t *clock = (test_clock_t *)user;
    return (clock == NULL) ? 0u : clock->monotonic_ms;
}

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static bool dt_clock_wall(void *user, uint64_t *out)
{
    test_clock_t *clock = (test_clock_t *)user;
    if (clock == NULL || out == NULL) {
        return false;
    }
    *out = clock->wall_ms;
    return true;
}

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static locker_status_t dt_clock_uuid(void *user, locker_uuid_t *out)
{
    test_clock_t *clock = (test_clock_t *)user;
    if (clock == NULL || out == NULL) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    clock->uuid_counter += 1u;
    uint32_t value = clock->uuid_counter;
    memset(out->bytes, 0, sizeof(out->bytes));
    out->bytes[0] = (uint8_t)(value & 0xffu);
    out->bytes[1] = (uint8_t)((value >> 8u) & 0xffu);
    out->bytes[2] = (uint8_t)((value >> 16u) & 0xffu);
    out->bytes[3] = (uint8_t)((value >> 24u) & 0xffu);
    out->bytes[4] = 0x9au;
    out->bytes[5] = 0x5bu;
    out->bytes[6] = (uint8_t)(0x40u | (out->bytes[6] & 0x0fu));
    out->bytes[7] = 0x8cu;
    out->bytes[8] = 0x80u;
    out->bytes[9] = 0x1du;
    out->bytes[15] = (uint8_t)(value & 0xffu);
    return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
}

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static locker_clock_port_t dt_clock_port(test_clock_t *clock)
{
    locker_clock_port_t port;
    memset(&port, 0, sizeof(port));
    port.user = clock;
    port.monotonic_ms = dt_clock_monotonic;
    port.wall_utc_ms = dt_clock_wall;
    port.new_uuid = dt_clock_uuid;
    return port;
}

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static void dt_uuid_fill(locker_uuid_t *uuid, uint8_t seed)
{
    uint32_t i;
    for (i = 0; i < sizeof(uuid->bytes); i++) {
        uuid->bytes[i] = (uint8_t)(seed + i);
    }
    uuid->bytes[6] = (uint8_t)(0x40u | (uuid->bytes[6] & 0x0fu));
    uuid->bytes[8] = (uint8_t)(0x80u | (uuid->bytes[8] & 0x3fu));
}

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static void dt_uuid_text(const locker_uuid_t *uuid, char *out, size_t out_size)
{
    if (locker_uuid_format(uuid, out, out_size) != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        if (out_size > 0u) {
            out[0] = '\0';
        }
    }
}

/* ---------- 假仓储 ---------- */

typedef struct dt_doc {
    bool used;
    char collection[LOCKER_DOMAIN_COLLECTION_TEXT];
    char key[LOCKER_DOMAIN_KEY_TEXT];
    char value[DT_VALUE_BYTES];
} dt_doc_t;

struct locker_repo {
    dt_doc_t docs[DT_MAX_DOCS];
    uint64_t revision;
    locker_status_t query_status;
    locker_status_t commit_status;
    locker_status_t import_status;
    locker_status_t recovery_status;
    uint32_t query_calls;
    uint32_t commit_calls;
    uint32_t import_calls;
    uint32_t recovery_calls;
    uint32_t last_import_count;
    bool last_import_gap;
    bool last_import_has_projection;
    char last_change[65536];
    size_t last_change_length;
    locker_event_key_t last_committed;
    locker_json_node_t pool_nodes[DT_POOL_NODES];
    locker_json_member_t pool_members[DT_POOL_MEMBERS];
};

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static dt_doc_t *dt_repo_find(struct locker_repo *repo, const char *collection,
                                                 const char *key)
{
    uint32_t i;
    if (repo == NULL || collection == NULL || key == NULL) {
        return NULL;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < DT_MAX_DOCS; i++) {
        if (!repo->docs[i].used) {
            continue;
        }
        if (strcmp(repo->docs[i].collection, collection) != 0) {
            continue;
        }
        if (strcmp(repo->docs[i].key, key) == 0) {
            return &repo->docs[i];
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static bool dt_repo_put(struct locker_repo *repo, const char *collection,
                                           const char *key, const char *value)
{
    dt_doc_t *doc = dt_repo_find(repo, collection, key);
    uint32_t i;
    if (doc == NULL) {
        for (i = 0; i < DT_MAX_DOCS; i++) {
            if (!repo->docs[i].used) {
                doc = &repo->docs[i];
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    if (doc == NULL) {
        return false;
    }
    if (strlen(collection) >= sizeof(doc->collection) || strlen(key) >= sizeof(doc->key) ||
        strlen(value) >= sizeof(doc->value)) {
        return false;
    }
    memset(doc, 0, sizeof(*doc));
    doc->used = true;
    memcpy(doc->collection, collection, strlen(collection) + 1u);
    memcpy(doc->key, key, strlen(key) + 1u);
    memcpy(doc->value, value, strlen(value) + 1u);
    return true;
}

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static bool dt_repo_delete(struct locker_repo *repo, const char *collection,
                                              const char *key)
{
    dt_doc_t *doc = dt_repo_find(repo, collection, key);
    if (doc == NULL) {
        return true;
    }
    memset(doc, 0, sizeof(*doc));
    return true;
}

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static bool dt_node_text(const locker_json_document_t *doc,
                                            const locker_json_node_t *node, char *out,
                                            size_t out_size)
{
    size_t length = 0;
    if (node == NULL || out == NULL || out_size == 0u) {
        return false;
    }
    if (!locker_domain_json_text(doc, node, out, out_size - 1u, &length)) {
        return false;
    }
    out[length] = '\0';
    return true;
}

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static bool dt_read_u64_text(const locker_json_document_t *doc,
                                                const locker_json_node_t *node, uint64_t *out)
{
    char text[LOCKER_DOMAIN_U64_TEXT];
    if (!dt_node_text(doc, node, text, sizeof(text))) {
        return false;
    }
    return locker_u64_parse_decimal(text, strlen(text), out) == LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
}

/* 按 (collection, key) 取下一个大于 after_key 的键；after_key 为 NULL 表示从头。 */
LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static bool dt_repo_next_key(struct locker_repo *repo, const char *collection,
                                                const char *after_key, char *out, size_t out_size)
{
    bool found = false;
    uint32_t i;
    for (i = 0; i < DT_MAX_DOCS; i++) {
        if (!repo->docs[i].used) {
            continue;
        }
        if (strcmp(repo->docs[i].collection, collection) != 0) {
            continue;
        }
        if (after_key != NULL && after_key[0] != '\0' &&
            strcmp(repo->docs[i].key, after_key) <= 0) {
            continue;
        }
        if (!found || strcmp(repo->docs[i].key, out) < 0) {
            memcpy(out, repo->docs[i].key, strlen(repo->docs[i].key) + 1u);
            found = true;
        }
    }
    (void)out_size;
    return found;
}

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static void dt_append_doc(locker_json_writer_t *writer, const dt_doc_t *doc,
                                             uint32_t *included)
{
    if (*included > 0u) {
        locker_domain_json_literal(writer, ",");
    }
    locker_domain_json_literal(writer, "{\"collection\":");
    locker_domain_json_string(writer, doc->collection);
    locker_domain_json_literal(writer, ",\"key\":");
    locker_domain_json_string(writer, doc->key);
    locker_domain_json_literal(writer, ",\"value\":");
    locker_domain_json_literal(writer, doc->value);
    locker_domain_json_literal(writer, "}");
    *included += 1u;
}

/* ---------- 夹具 ---------- */

typedef struct dt_fixture {
    test_clock_t clock;
    locker_repo_t *repo;
    locker_device_t *device;
    locker_publisher_t *publisher;
    locker_core_t *core;
} dt_fixture_t;

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static bool dt_fixture_init(dt_fixture_t *fixture, bool with_device)
{
    locker_core_config_t config;
    locker_status_t status;

    memset(fixture, 0, sizeof(*fixture));
    fixture->clock.monotonic_ms = 1000u;
    /* 墙钟固定为 2025-10-21T00:00:00Z：所有时间戳断言都按这个基准写。 */
    fixture->clock.wall_ms = 1761004800000ull;
    fixture->clock.uuid_counter = 0u;
    fixture->repo = (locker_repo_t *)calloc(1u, sizeof(struct locker_repo));
    fixture->publisher = (locker_publisher_t *)calloc(1u, sizeof(struct locker_publisher));
    if (fixture->repo == NULL || fixture->publisher == NULL) {
        return false;
    }
    if (with_device) {
        fixture->device = (locker_device_t *)calloc(1u, sizeof(struct locker_device));
        if (fixture->device == NULL) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    memset(&config, 0, sizeof(config));
    memcpy(config.cabinet_name, "test-cabinet", sizeof("test-cabinet"));
    dt_uuid_fill(&config.instance_id, 0x40u);
    config.clock = dt_clock_port(&fixture->clock);
    config.queue_capacity = 0u;
    config.completion_retention_ms = LOCKER_COMPLETION_RETENTION_MS;
    status = locker_core_create(&config, fixture->repo, fixture->device, fixture->publisher,
                                &fixture->core);
    return status == LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
}

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static void dt_fixture_free(dt_fixture_t *fixture)
{
    if (fixture->core != NULL) {
        locker_core_destroy(fixture->core);
        fixture->core = NULL;
    }
    free(fixture->repo);
    free(fixture->device);
    free(fixture->publisher);
    fixture->repo = NULL;
    fixture->device = NULL;
    fixture->publisher = NULL;
}

/* 把 Core 直接推到「已恢复 + 设备就绪」：单测只验证状态机，不重复跑恢复路径。 */
LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static void dt_core_ready(dt_fixture_t *fixture)
{
    locker_core_t *core = fixture->core;
    core->phase = DOMAIN_PHASE_READY;
    core->recovery_done = true;
    core->recovery_status = LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
    core->recovery_ready = true;
    core->now_ms = fixture->clock.monotonic_ms;
    core->next_action_no = 1u;
    core->action_watermark = 0u;
    /* 单柜格模型里工具是设备配置的一部分，真实恢复路径由 recover_tools 从
       tools 集合填好。create 现在要求工具可知且版本匹配（审计 R1-B-9），
       所以夹具也要给出这一份投影，否则每个申请都会卡在 NOT_READY。
       需要验证「工具不可知」的用例自行把它改回 false。 */
    dt_uuid_fill(&core->tool_id, 0x22u);
    core->tool_revision = 1u;
    domain_text_copy(core->tool_name, sizeof(core->tool_name), "柜内工具");
    core->tool_availability = LOCKER_TOOL_AVAILABILITY_AVAILABLE;
    core->tool_valid = true;
    core->loan_open = false;
    core->active.used = false;
    /* 时钟换算样本：真实路径由 domain_anchor_request 周期性采集，单测不跑
       设备环，所以要给一份等价样本。偏移取 0，于是 MCU 时钟与主机单调时钟
       同值，测试里比较 not_after_mcu_ms 时不用再算一遍换算。
       验证「锚点缺失一律拒发」的用例自行清 anchor_valid。 */
    core->anchor_valid = true;
    core->anchor_offset_low_ms = 0;
    core->anchor_offset_high_ms = 0;
    core->anchor_sampled_ms = fixture->clock.monotonic_ms;
}

LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static void dt_core_device_ready(dt_fixture_t *fixture, uint64_t boot_id,
                                                    uint8_t door, uint8_t item)
{
    locker_core_t *core = fixture->core;
    dt_core_ready(fixture);
    core->device_session_up = true;
    core->device_snapshot_valid = true;
    core->session.valid = true;
    dt_uuid_fill(&core->session.session_id, 0x10u);
    dt_uuid_fill(&core->session.journal_epoch, 0x20u);
    core->session.boot_id = boot_id;
    memcpy(core->session.device_id, DT_DEVICE_ID, sizeof(DT_DEVICE_ID));
    core->session.device_mode = LOCKER_DEVICE_MODE_READY;
    core->session.journal_state = LOCKER_WIRE_WIRE_JOURNAL_STATE_HEALTHY;
    core->session.capabilities = 0u;
    core->session.action_watermark = 0u;
    core->cursor.valid = true;
    core->cursor.journal_epoch = core->session.journal_epoch;
    memcpy(core->cursor.device_id, DT_DEVICE_ID, sizeof(DT_DEVICE_ID));
    /* through 是完整 EventKey：补取请求把它原样当 after 传给设备端口，
       缺 device_id/journal_epoch 会让设备侧拿到残缺游标。 */
    memcpy(core->cursor.through.device_id, DT_DEVICE_ID, sizeof(DT_DEVICE_ID));
    core->cursor.through.journal_epoch = core->session.journal_epoch;
    core->cursor.through.boot_id = boot_id;
    core->cursor.through.event_seq = 0u;
    core->cursor.continuity_ok = true;
    core->fetch_gap = false;
    /* 新的设备上下文没有已提交水位，也没有待确认水位。 */
    memset(&core->import_cursor, 0, sizeof(core->import_cursor));
    core->import_cursor_valid = false;
    core->import_continuity = false;
    memset(&core->ack_cursor, 0, sizeof(core->ack_cursor));
    core->ack_cursor_valid = false;
    core->physical.valid = true;
    memcpy(core->physical.device_id, DT_DEVICE_ID, sizeof(DT_DEVICE_ID));
    core->physical.journal_epoch = core->session.journal_epoch;
    core->physical.boot_id = boot_id;
    core->physical.physical_revision = 7u;
    core->physical.door = door;
    core->physical.item = item;
    core->physical.supply_valid = true;
    core->physical.supply_mv = 12000u;
    core->physical.observed_mono_ms = core->now_ms;
    core->physical.time_quality = LOCKER_TIME_QUALITY_HOST_SYNCED;
    core->physical.continuity_ok = true;
#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L

/*
 * S1-04 业务核心：命令侧（申请 / 取消 / 确认 / 核验 / 柜前证明 / outbox / 超时）。
 *
 * 这里钉死的规则（对应任务卡与 internal-interfaces.md）：
 *   1. 两申请竞争只有一条活路：后到的明确 BUSY，不排队、不覆盖前者。
 *   2. 只有本人能确认/取消自己的操作；错误用户一律 INVALID。
 *   3. 提前确认（还在 REQUESTED/ACCESS_ACTIVE）一律 CONFLICT，不能跳步。
 *   4. 确认要同时满足物理终态与 item_transition_seen，缺一个就进 REVIEW_REQUIRED。
 *   5. 可能已执行的动作，取消只是核验请求（REVIEW_REQUIRED + 响应 CONFLICT）。
 *   6. 核验（resolve）只有 ADMIN、必须匹配 kind、门必须闭、物品必须明确。
 *   7. 到柜证明 15 秒过期：过期后重新进 REVIEW_REQUIRED，不能拿旧证明开门。
 *   8. outbox 先落「已尝试发送」检查点再发；检查点失败绝不发送。
 *   9. 发送后结果未知只查不重发；端口 BUSY 不算失败。
 *  10. 所有超时都有确定出口，且不会把 REVIEW_REQUIRED 再推回去。
 */

#include "domain_test_support.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

/* ---------- 命令/查询执行辅助 ---------- */

static locker_command_t dc_command(const locker_principal_t *principal, const char *kind, const char *arguments,
                                   uint8_t request_seed, uint8_t idem_seed)
{
    locker_command_t command;
    memset(&command, 0, sizeof(command));
    command.principal = *principal;
    dt_uuid_fill(&command.request_id, request_seed);
    if (idem_seed != 0u) {
        dt_uuid_fill(&command.idempotency_key, idem_seed);
    }
    (void)snprintf(command.kind, sizeof(command.kind), "%s", kind);
    (void)snprintf(command.arguments_json, sizeof(command.arguments_json), "%s", arguments);
    return command;
}

/* 跑一条命令并取回终态；返回领域状态（不是 IO 状态）。 */
static locker_status_t dc_run_command(dt_fixture_t *fixture, const locker_command_t *command,
                                      locker_query_result_t *result)
{
    domain_job_t job;
    locker_uuid_t token;
    uint32_t slot;

    memset(&job, 0, sizeof(job));
    memset(result, 0, sizeof(*result));
    slot = domain_completion_acquire(fixture->core, &token);
    TEST_CHECK(slot != UINT32_MAX);
    if (slot == UINT32_MAX) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_IO_ERROR;
    }
    job.kind = (uint8_t)DOMAIN_JOB_COMMAND;
    job.completion = slot;
    job.body.command = *command;
    domain_handle_command(fixture->core, &job);
    if (domain_completion_wait_final(fixture->core, slot, 0u, result) != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        domain_completion_release(fixture->core, slot);
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    domain_completion_release(fixture->core, slot);
    return result->status;
}

static locker_status_t dc_run_query(dt_fixture_t *fixture, const locker_query_t *query,
                                    locker_query_result_t *result)
{
    domain_job_t job;
    locker_uuid_t token;
    uint32_t slot;

    memset(&job, 0, sizeof(job));
    memset(result, 0, sizeof(*result));
    slot = domain_completion_acquire(fixture->core, &token);
    TEST_CHECK(slot != UINT32_MAX);
    if (slot == UINT32_MAX) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_IO_ERROR;
    }
    job.kind = (uint8_t)DOMAIN_JOB_QUERY;
    job.completion = slot;
    job.body.query = *query;
    domain_handle_query(fixture->core, &job);
    if (domain_completion_wait_final(fixture->core, slot, 0u, result) != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        domain_completion_release(fixture->core, slot);
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    domain_completion_release(fixture->core, slot);
    return result->status;
}

/*
 * 响应必须是契约里的信封：{data, snapshot_revision, request_id}——
 * 顶层恰好三个成员，data 是一个完整的 JSON 值。
 *
 * 这条断言是补漏来的：以前这里只对 json 做子串匹配
 * （test_text_contains），所以「多一层大括号」这种断头 JSON
 * （{"data":{{"id":…）在单测里永远是绿的，直到集成测试解析响应
 * 才炸出来。凡是拿到 result->json 的地方都应该先过这里。
 */
static bool dc_envelope_ok(dt_fixture_t *fixture, const locker_query_result_t *result)

/* 造一条已落库的操作并装进 core->active（等价于恢复期走的那条路径）。 */
static bool dc_seed_active(dt_fixture_t *fixture, uint8_t kind, uint8_t state, uint64_t revision,
                           const char *updated_at, const locker_uuid_t *id, const locker_uuid_t *tool_id,
                           const locker_uuid_t *actor_id)
{
    domain_operation_t operation;

    if (!dt_seed_operation(fixture, id, tool_id, actor_id, kind, state, revision, updated_at)) {
        return false;
    }
    memset(&operation, 0, sizeof(operation));
    if (domain_operation_load(fixture->core, id, 0, &operation) != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        return false;
    }
    fixture->core->active = operation;
    return true;
}

static void dc_build_create_args(char *out, size_t out_size, const locker_uuid_t *tool_id, const char *kind,
{
    locker_principal_t principal;
    memset(&principal, 0, sizeof(principal));
    dt_uuid_fill(&principal.user_id, seed);
    dt_uuid_fill(&principal.session_id, (uint8_t)(seed + 0x10u));
    principal.role = role;
    principal.authenticated = true;
    return principal;
}

/* 一条已提交的操作记录（用于加载路径），state 用枚举序号。 */
LOCKER_TEST_HELPER static bool dt_seed_operation(dt_fixture_t *fixture, const locker_uuid_t *id,
                                                 const locker_uuid_t *tool_id,
                                                 const locker_uuid_t *actor_id, uint8_t kind,
                                                 uint8_t state, uint64_t revision,
                                                 const char *updated_at)
{
    char key[LOCKER_DOMAIN_UUID_TEXT];
    char tool[LOCKER_DOMAIN_UUID_TEXT];
    char actor[LOCKER_DOMAIN_UUID_TEXT];
    char value[4096];
    locker_json_writer_t writer;
    char revision_text[LOCKER_DOMAIN_U64_TEXT];

    dt_uuid_text(id, key, sizeof(key));
    dt_uuid_text(tool_id, tool, sizeof(tool));
    dt_uuid_text(actor_id, actor, sizeof(actor));
    if (key[0] == '\0' || tool[0] == '\0' || actor[0] == '\0') {
        return false;
    }
    if (locker_u64_format_decimal(revision, revision_text, sizeof(revision_text)) !=
        LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        return false;
    }

    locker_domain_json_writer_init(&writer, value, sizeof(value));
    locker_domain_json_literal(&writer, "{\"operation\":{\"id\":");
    locker_domain_json_string(&writer, key);
    locker_domain_json_literal(&writer, ",\"tool_id\":");
    locker_domain_json_string(&writer, tool);
    locker_domain_json_literal(&writer, ",\"kind\":");
    locker_domain_json_string(&writer, domain_kind_name(kind));
    locker_domain_json_literal(&writer, ",\"actor_id\":");
    locker_domain_json_string(&writer, actor);
    locker_domain_json_literal(&writer, ",\"state\":");
    locker_domain_json_string(&writer, domain_state_name(state));
    locker_domain_json_literal(&writer, ",\"operation_revision\":");
    locker_domain_json_string(&writer, revision_text);
    locker_domain_json_literal(&writer, ",\"command_id\":null,\"reason_code\":null,\"created_at\":");
    locker_domain_json_string(&writer, updated_at);
    locker_domain_json_literal(&writer, ",\"updated_at\":");
    locker_domain_json_string(&writer, updated_at);
    locker_domain_json_literal(&writer, ",\"allowed_actions\":[],\"evidence\":{");
    locker_domain_json_literal(
        &writer,
        "\"history_complete\":true,\"door_open_seen\":false,\"item_transition_seen\":false,"
        "\"physical\":null,\"source_events\":[],\"manual_resolution\":false}},");
    locker_domain_json_literal(&writer, "\"baseline\":");
    locker_domain_json_literal(&writer, "{\"device_id\":\"" DT_DEVICE_ID "\",\"journal_epoch\":");
    locker_domain_json_string(&writer, "00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000000");
    locker_domain_json_literal(
        &writer,
        ",\"boot_id\":\"1\",\"physical_revision\":\"7\",\"door\":\"CLOSED\",\"item\":\"PRESENT\","
        "\"supply_mv\":12000,\"observed_age_ms\":0,\"time_quality\":\"HOST_SYNCED\","
        "\"continuity_ok\":true,\"dropped_events\":\"0\",\"durable_cursor\":{"
        "\"device_id\":\"" DT_DEVICE_ID "\",\"journal_epoch\":");
    locker_domain_json_string(&writer, "00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000000");
    locker_domain_json_literal(&writer, ",\"boot_id\":\"1\",\"event_seq\":\"0\"}},");
    locker_domain_json_literal(
        &writer, "\"related_loan_id\":null,\"proof_source\":null,\"proof_consumed\":false}");
    if (!locker_domain_json_writer_ok(&writer)) {
        return false;
    }
    return dt_repo_put(fixture->repo, "operations", key, value);
}

#endif /* DOMAIN_TEST_SUPPORT_H */
#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L

/*
 * S1-04 单元测试：设备桥（domain_device.c）与设备事件/响应入口
 * （domain_commands.c:974-1006）。
 *
 * 钉死的东西：
 *   1. 周期泵的三重门：100 ms 轮询间隔、500 ms 快照、200 ms 补取、1000 ms 健康；
 *      会话未建立时一个请求都不发（不能对着没握手的设备刷帧）。
 *   2. 事件只接受当前 journal_epoch 且严格大于已应用水位；重复/旧 epoch/跨 boot
 *      回退都不得改动内存状态，更不能推进操作。
 *   3. 只有 PULSE_STARTED 能把 ACCESS_PENDING 推到 ACCESS_ACTIVE；EXECUTE 回复里的
 *      ACCEPTED 不推进（契约 :221「ACK 不是开门」）。
 *   4. 缺口是两个独立标志：cursor.continuity_ok=false 表示「事件流不连续」，
 *      fetch_gap=true 表示「必须补取」。补传事件、CONTINUITY_LOST、JOURNAL_FAULT、
 *      快照 flags bit2 都必须同时置位。
 *   5. sink 回调跑在设备 reader 线程：环满只锁存溢出标志并返回 OK，绝不返回
 *      NO_SPACE（那会让设备侧 shutdown 会话，见 host/device/README.md:81）。
 *   6. 历史读卡（live_transport=false）只补记录，不产生柜前证明。
 */

#include "domain_test_support.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

/* ---------- 小工具 ---------- */

/* 一条属于当前会话的事件；supply 用 0xffffffff 表示「本事件不带供电读数」。 */
static locker_device_event_t dv_event(const locker_core_t *core, uint16_t type, uint64_t seq,
                                      bool live)
{
    locker_device_event_t event;
    memset(&event, 0, sizeof(event));
    memcpy(event.key.device_id, DT_DEVICE_ID, sizeof(DT_DEVICE_ID));
    event.key.journal_epoch = core->cursor.journal_epoch;
    event.key.boot_id = core->cursor.through.boot_id;
    event.key.event_seq = seq;
    event.mono_ms = 100u + seq;
    event.physical_revision = 7u + seq;
    event.event_type = type;
    event.reason = 0u;
    event.flags = 0u;
    event.door = LOCKER_DOOR_STATE_OPEN;
    event.item = LOCKER_ITEM_STATE_PRESENT;
    event.supply_mv = 0xffffffffu;
    event.card_uid_len = 0u;
    event.live_transport = live;
    return event;
}

/* 在 core->active 里放一个可持久化的操作（baseline 必须有，否则写手会置 failed）。 */
static void dv_arm_operation(locker_core_t *core, uint8_t kind, uint8_t state, uint8_t id_seed)
{
    memset(&core->active, 0, sizeof(core->active));
    core->active.used = true;
    dt_uuid_fill(&core->active.id, id_seed);
    dt_uuid_fill(&core->active.tool_id, (uint8_t)(id_seed + 1u));
    dt_uuid_fill(&core->active.actor_id, (uint8_t)(id_seed + 2u));
    core->active.kind = kind;
    core->active.state = state;
    core->active.revision = 1u;
    memcpy(core->active.created_at, "2026-10-26T00:00:00Z", sizeof("2026-10-26T00:00:00Z"));
    memcpy(core->active.updated_at, "2026-10-26T00:00:00Z", sizeof("2026-10-26T00:00:00Z"));
    core->active.history_complete = true;
    core->active.has_baseline = true;
    core->active.baseline = core->physical;
}

/* 一条属于 core->active 的事件（operation_id 匹配，才会计入 source_events）。 */
static void dv_attach_active(locker_core_t *core, locker_device_event_t *event)
{
    event->operation_id = core->active.id;
}

/* ---------- 在途请求槽 ---------- */

    principal = dt_principal(LOCKER_ROLE_USER, 0x70u);
    TEST_CHECK(dt_seed_principal(&fixture, &principal));
    admin = dt_principal(LOCKER_ROLE_ADMIN, 0x80u);
    TEST_CHECK(dt_seed_principal(&fixture, &admin));

    /* 认证端口在 S1-04 只是占位：明确 NOT_READY，不伪造成功。 */
    slot = domain_completion_acquire(fixture.core, &token);
    TEST_CHECK(slot != UINT32_MAX);
    memset(&job, 0, sizeof(job));
    job.kind = (uint8_t)DOMAIN_JOB_AUTH;
    job.completion = slot;
    (void)snprintf(job.body.auth.token, sizeof(job.body.auth.token), "%s", "not-a-real-token");
    job.body.auth.token_length = strlen(job.body.auth.token);
    domain_handle_auth(fixture.core, &job);
    {
        locker_query_result_t result;
        memset(&result, 0, sizeof(result));
        TEST_CHECK_STATUS(LOCKER_STATUS_OK, domain_completion_wait_final(fixture.core, slot, 0u, &result));
        TEST_CHECK(result.status == LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY);
    }
    domain_completion_release(fixture.core, slot);

    /* 维护核验：非 ADMIN 静默拒绝，不发 resume；ADMIN + 匹配的版本才放行一次。 */
    memset(&job, 0, sizeof(job));
    job.kind = (uint8_t)DOMAIN_JOB_MAINTENANCE;
    job.completion = UINT32_MAX;
    job.body.maintenance.peer.verified_admin_peer = true;
    job.body.maintenance.maintenance.principal = principal;
    job.body.maintenance.maintenance.physical_revision = 7u;
    job.body.maintenance.maintenance.snapshot_revision = fixture.core->revision;
    (void)snprintf(job.body.maintenance.maintenance.reason, sizeof(job.body.maintenance.maintenance.reason), "%s",
                   "reconcile after restart");
    domain_handle_maintenance(fixture.core, &job);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(0u, (uint64_t)fixture.device->resume_calls);

    job.body.maintenance.maintenance.principal = admin;
    domain_handle_maintenance(fixture.core, &job);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(1u, (uint64_t)fixture.device->resume_calls);
    TEST_CHECK(locker_uuid_equal(&fixture.device->last_guard.session_id, &fixture.core->session.session_id));
    TEST_CHECK_U64(fixture.core->session.boot_id, fixture.device->last_guard.expected_boot_id);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(7u, fixture.device->last_guard.expected_physical_revision);

    /* 版本不符不发。 */
    job.body.maintenance.maintenance.physical_revision = 9u;
    domain_handle_maintenance(fixture.core, &job);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(1u, (uint64_t)fixture.device->resume_calls);

    /* tick：推进时钟、跑超时、泵设备、发布进度，但不越权改状态。 */
    {
        uint64_t progress_before = fixture.core->progress_counter;
        memset(&job, 0, sizeof(job));
        job.kind = (uint8_t)DOMAIN_JOB_TICK;
        job.completion = UINT32_MAX;
        job.body.tick_ms = 1000u;
        domain_handle_tick(fixture.core, job.body.tick_ms);
        TEST_CHECK(fixture.core->progress_counter > progress_before);
        TEST_CHECK(fixture.core->progress_valid);
        TEST_CHECK(fixture.core->progress.target == LOCKER_PROGRESS_TARGET_SERVICE);
        TEST_CHECK(locker_uuid_equal(&fixture.core->progress.instance_id, &fixture.core->instance_id));
        TEST_CHECK_U64(fixture.core->progress_counter, fixture.core->progress.progress);

        /* 相同 counter 重复读取不续命：progress 只在 tick 里更新。 */
        {
            locker_progress_t snapshot;
            memset(&snapshot, 0, sizeof(snapshot));
            TEST_CHECK_STATUS(LOCKER_STATUS_OK, locker_core_get_progress(fixture.core, &snapshot));
            TEST_CHECK_U64(fixture.core->progress.progress, snapshot.progress);
            memset(&snapshot, 0, sizeof(snapshot));
            TEST_CHECK_STATUS(LOCKER_STATUS_OK, locker_core_get_progress(fixture.core, &snapshot));
            TEST_CHECK_U64(fixture.core->progress.progress, snapshot.progress);
        }
    }

    /* 未知作业类型不动状态。 */
    {
        uint64_t revision_before = fixture.core->revision;
        memset(&job, 0, sizeof(job));
        job.kind = 200u;
        job.completion = UINT32_MAX;
        domain_handle_job(fixture.core, &job);
        TEST_CHECK_U64(revision_before, fixture.core->revision);
    }

    dt_fixture_free(&fixture);
}

+/* observed_age_ms 是**公开**的新鲜度声明，必须由 Core 按自己的单调时钟算出来：
+   写死 0 会让离线很久的缓存快照在投影里看起来像刚采到的，管理员据此改账就等于
+   用过期物理事实冒充当前事实（审计 R1-B-12）。未采样（observed_mono_ms==0 或
+   now 早于采样点）时不声称新鲜，写 uint32 上限表示「未知」，与
+   ProgressReport.observed_age_ms 的既有语义一致。 */
+static uint32_t physical_observed_age_ms(const domain_physical_t *physical, uint64_t now_ms)
+{
+    if (physical->observed_mono_ms == 0u || now_ms < physical->observed_mono_ms) {
+        return UINT32_MAX;
+    }
+    uint64_t age = now_ms - physical->observed_mono_ms;
+    if (age > (uint64_t)UINT32_MAX) {
+        return UINT32_MAX;
+    }
+    return (uint32_t)age;
+}

  *
  * 本文件只处理 loans 集合与 core->loan_* 内存副本；operations 的
  * related_loan_id 由 domain_loan_prepare 在终结事务之前写入。
+ *
+ * **终结与账本必须同一个事务**：先 `domain_loan_check` 写条件、再
+ * `domain_loan_apply` 写变更，两者都挂在调用方的 change 上，由调用方连同
+ * 操作状态一起提交。分两次提交会在中途失败时留下「操作 COMPLETED 但 loans
+ * 没动」的半截状态——借出侧账上说已借出却没有有效借用、归还侧账上已完成但
+ * 借用仍 open，正是 x-rules 第 7 条与
+ * `docs/architecture/reviews/interface-expert.md:29` 要求排除的情形。
+ *
+ * RepositoryChange 的 checks 段必须在 mutations 段之前一次性写完
+ * （见 domain_change.c 的 domain_change_check 约束），所以这里严格分成
+ * check / apply 两个阶段，不能合成一次调用。
  */

+void domain_response_end(locker_json_writer_t *writer, uint64_t revision, const locker_uuid_t *request_id)
+{
+    if (writer == NULL || !locker_domain_json_writer_ok(writer)) {
+        return;
+    }
+    /* 用于「data 对象由 domain_response_data_object 打开、调用方只写成员」的形态：
+       这里先补一个 `}` 关闭 data 对象，再追加信封其余成员。调用方此时**不能**
+       自己再写一个 `}` 来关 data 对象，否则会得到
+       `{"data":{"items":[]}},"snapshot_revision":…`（集成测试实测的非法 JSON）。 */
+    locker_domain_json_literal(writer, "}");
+    domain_response_tail(writer, revision, request_id);
+}
+
+void domain_response_end_value(locker_json_writer_t *writer, uint64_t revision,
+                               const locker_uuid_t *request_id)
+{
+    if (writer == NULL || !locker_domain_json_writer_ok(writer)) {
+        return;
+    }
+    /* 用于「调用方已经写出一个完整 JSON 值」的形态：投影写手
+       domain_write_operation_projection / domain_write_device_projection /
+       write_tool_object 自己就写出 `{…}` 或 null。这里不能再补 `}`，
+       也不能再调 domain_response_data_object（会得到 `{"data":{{"id":…`）。 */
+    domain_response_tail(writer, revision, request_id);
+}

 void domain_publish(locker_core_t *core, const locker_uuid_t *operation_id, const char *resource,
#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L

#include "domain_internal.h"

#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

/* 仓储访问：全部在 Core 线程上同步调用（module-ownership.md:49）。 */

static locker_status_t doc_query(locker_core_t *core, int slot, const char *collection, const char *key,
                                 uint16_t limit, const char *after_key, locker_repo_result_t *result)
{
    if (core == NULL || core->repo == NULL || collection == NULL) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    if (slot < 0 || slot > 1) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    memset(result, 0, sizeof(*result));
    locker_repo_query_t query;
    memset(&query, 0, sizeof(query));
    query.schema_version = 1u;
    if (key != NULL) {
        if (strlen(key) >= sizeof(query.key)) {
            return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
        }
        memcpy(query.key, key, strlen(key) + 1u);
        query.limit = 1u;
        query.after_key[0] = '\0';
    } else {
        query.key[0] = '\0';
        if (after_key != NULL) {
            if (strlen(after_key) >= sizeof(query.after_key)) {
                return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
            }
            memcpy(query.after_key, after_key, strlen(after_key) + 1u);
        }
        query.limit = limit;
    }
    if (strlen(collection) >= sizeof(query.collection)) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    memcpy(query.collection, collection, strlen(collection) + 1u);
    (void)slot;
    return locker_repo_query(core->repo, &query, result);
}

/* 解析查询结果，把 slot 槽的缓冲交给 doc->doc；doc->value 指向 documents[0].value。 */
static bool doc_parse(locker_core_t *core, int slot, locker_repo_result_t *result, domain_doc_t *out)
{
    memset(out, 0, sizeof(*out));
    out->doc.nodes = core->doc_nodes[slot];
    out->doc.node_capacity = LOCKER_DOMAIN_DOC_NODES;
    out->doc.members = core->doc_members[slot];
    out->doc.member_capacity = LOCKER_DOMAIN_DOC_MEMBERS;
    out->revision = result->snapshot_revision;
    if (!locker_domain_json_parse(&out->doc, result->json, result->length)) {
        return false;
    }
    const locker_json_node_t *documents = locker_domain_json_get(&out->doc, locker_domain_json_root(&out->doc), "docume
nts");
    if (documents == NULL || documents->type != LOCKER_JSON_ARRAY) {
        return false;
    }
    if (documents->count == 0u) {
        out->found = false;
        out->value = NULL;
        return true;
    }
    const locker_json_node_t *document = locker_domain_json_at(&out->doc, documents, 0u);
    if (document == NULL) {
        return false;
    }
    const locker_json_node_t *value = locker_domain_json_get(&out->doc, document, "value");
    if (value == NULL) {
        return false;
    }
    out->found = true;
    out->value = value;
    return true;
}

locker_status_t domain_repo_doc(locker_core_t *core, int slot, const char *collection, const char *key,
                                domain_doc_t *out)
{
    if (out == NULL) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    locker_repo_result_t *result = &core->repo_result;
    locker_status_t status = doc_query(core, slot, collection, key, 1u, NULL, result);
    if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        return status;
    }
    if (!doc_parse(core, slot, result, out)) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_IO_ERROR;
    }
    return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
}

locker_status_t domain_repo_page(locker_core_t *core, const char *collection, const char *after_key,
                                 uint16_t limit, domain_doc_t *out)
{
    if (out == NULL) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    memset(out, 0, sizeof(*out));
    locker_repo_query_t query;
    memset(&query, 0, sizeof(query));
    query.schema_version = 1u;
    if (strlen(collection) >= sizeof(query.collection)) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    memcpy(query.collection, collection, strlen(collection) + 1u);
    query.key[0] = '\0';
    if (after_key != NULL) {
        if (strlen(after_key) >= sizeof(query.after_key)) {
            return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
        }
        memcpy(query.after_key, after_key, strlen(after_key) + 1u);
    }
    query.limit = limit;
    locker_repo_result_t *result = &core->repo_result;
    memset(result, 0, sizeof(*result));
    locker_status_t status = locker_repo_query(core->repo, &query, result);
    if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        return status;
    }
    out->doc.nodes = core->page_nodes;
    out->doc.node_capacity = LOCKER_DOMAIN_PAGE_NODES;
    out->doc.members = core->page_members;
    out->doc.member_capacity = LOCKER_DOMAIN_PAGE_MEMBERS;
    out->revision = result->snapshot_revision;
    if (!locker_domain_json_parse(&out->doc, result->json, result->length)) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_IO_ERROR;
    }
    out->found = true;
    out->value = locker_domain_json_root(&out->doc);
    return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
}

1690:     }

1692:     if (core->loan_open) {
1693:         write_loan_object(&writer, core);
1694:     }
1695:     /* 不在这里补 `}`：data 对象的关闭由 domain_response_end 负责。 */
1696:     locker_domain_json_literal(&writer, "]");
1697:     domain_response_end(&writer, core->revision, &query->request_id);
1698:     response_finish_json(core, slot, LOCKER_STATUS_OK, false, NULL, &writer);
1699:     return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
1700: }
1701: 
1702: void domain_handle_query(locker_core_t *core, const domain_job_t *job)
1703: {
1704:     const locker_query_t *query = &job->body.query;
1705:     uint32_t slot = job->completion;
1706:     locker_status_t status = domain_principal_check(core, &query->principal);
1707:     if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
1708:         response_finish(core, slot, status, false, NULL);
1709:         return;
1710:     }
1711:     if (strcmp(query->kind, LOCKER_CORE_DEVICE_GET) == 0) {
1712:         query_device_get(core, query, slot);
1713:     } else if (strcmp(query->kind, LOCKER_CORE_SNAPSHOT_GET) == 0) {
1714:         query_snapshot(core, query, slot);
1715:     } else if (strcmp(query->kind, LOCKER_CORE_TOOLS_LIST) == 0) {
1716:         query_tools(core, query, slot, false);
1717:     } else if (strcmp(query->kind, LOCKER_CORE_TOOL_GET) == 0) {
1718:         query_tools(core, query, slot, true);
1719:     } else if (strcmp(query->kind, LOCKER_CORE_OPERATION_GET) == 0) {
1720:         query_operation_get(core, query, slot);
1721:     } else if (strcmp(query->kind, LOCKER_CORE_LOANS_LIST) == 0) {
1722:         query_loans(core, query, slot);
1723:     } else if (strcmp(query->kind, LOCKER_CORE_REVIEWS_LIST) == 0) {
1724:         /* 核验队列是管理员视图（contract-source.json:1484-1487）：普通用户拿到它
1725:            等于能看到别人所有待核验的操作和原因码（审计 R1-B-13）。 */
1726:         if (query->principal.role != LOCKER_ROLE_ADMIN) {
1727:             response_finish(core, slot, LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID, false, NULL);
1728:             return;
1729:         }
1730:         query_list(core, query, slot, true);
1731:     } else if (strcmp(query->kind, LOCKER_CORE_EVENTS_LIST) == 0) {
1732:         /* 阶段 1 的 18 个仓储集合里没有 events：公开事件流由审计/设备事件派生，
1733:            归 S1-05 适配层；这里返回空页而不是伪造历史。 */
1734:         query_list(core, query, slot, false);
1735:     } else {
1736:         /* SESSION、USERS、CARD、VISION 四类命令归 S1-05 / S2：不空实现冒充。 */
1737:         response_finish(core, slot, LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY, false, NULL);
1738:     }
1739: }
1740: 
1741: /* ---------- 命令 ---------- */
1742: 
1743: void domain_handle_command(locker_core_t *core, const domain_job_t *job)
1744: {
1745:     const locker_command_t *command = &job->body.command;
1746:     uint32_t slot = job->completion;
1747:     locker_status_t status = domain_principal_check(core, &command->principal);
1748:     if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
1749:         response_finish(core, slot, status, false, NULL);
1750:         return;
1751:     }
1752:     if (core->phase != DOMAIN_PHASE_READY) {
1753:         response_finish(core, slot, LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY, false, NULL);
1754:         return;
1755:     }
1756:     /* 幂等：同一 key + 同一哈希返回原响应，不产生新 operation。 */
1757:     char request_hash[65];
1758:     if (strcmp(command->kind, LOCKER_CORE_OPERATION_CREATE) == 0 && locker_uuid_is_nil(&command->idempotency_key)
) {
1759:         response_finish(core, slot, LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID, false, NULL);
1760:         return;
1761:     }
1762:     if (!build_request_hash(core, command, request_hash, sizeof(request_hash))) {
1763:         response_finish(core, slot, LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID, false, NULL);
1764:         return;
1765:     }
1766:     locker_json_writer_t replay;
1767:     locker_domain_json_writer_init(&replay, core->response_buffer, sizeof(core->response_buffer));
1768:     domain_idempotency_result_t idem = idempotency_lookup(core, command, request_hash, &replay, NULL);
1769:     if (idem == DOMAIN_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT) {
1770:         response_finish(core, slot, LOCKER_STATUS_CONFLICT, false, NULL);
1771:         return;
1772:     }
1773:     if (idem == DOMAIN_IDEMPOTENCY_REPLAY) {
1774:         response_finish_json(core, slot, LOCKER_STATUS_OK, false, NULL, &replay);
1775:         return;
1776:     }
1777:     if (strcmp(command->kind, LOCKER_CORE_OPERATION_CREATE) == 0) {
1778:         command_operation_create(core, command, slot);
1779:     } else if (strcmp(command->kind, LOCKER_CORE_OPERATION_CONFIRM) == 0) {
1780:         command_operation_confirm(core, command, slot, request_hash);
1781:     } else if (strcmp(command->kind, LOCKER_CORE_OPERATION_CANCEL) == 0) {
1782:         command_operation_cancel(core, command, slot, request_hash);
1783:     } else if (strcmp(command->kind, LOCKER_CORE_OPERATION_RESOLVE) == 0) {
1784:         command_operation_resolve(core, command, slot, request_hash);
1785:     } else {
1786:         response_finish(core, slot, LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY, false, NULL);
1787:     }
1788: }
1789: 
1512:    "ToolRecord": {

exec
"C:\\WINDOWS\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command '[Console]::OutputEncoding=[System.Text.Encod
ing]::UTF8; Get-Content -Encoding UTF8 tests/unit/domain/test_domain_commands.c | Select-Object -Skip 977 -First 118; G
et-Content -Encoding UTF8 tests/unit/domain/test_domain_device.c | Select-Object -Skip 768 -First 49; Get-Content -Enco
