-s104-r201300-environment.log' in %REPO%
 succeeded in 196ms:
   以前只有 create 写幂等记录，同一个取消发两次，第二次会被状态机判成
   CONFLICT——客户端重试反而看到「第一次成功、第二次冲突」这种自相矛盾的
   结果，最坏的后果是操作者以为没生效而换个键再来一次。 */
static void test_action_idempotency(void)
{
    dt_fixture_t fixture;
    locker_principal_t principal;
    locker_query_result_t first;
    locker_query_result_t replay;
    locker_query_result_t conflict;
    locker_uuid_t id;
    locker_uuid_t tool;
    uint32_t commit_before;

    /* 确认：同键重放拿到逐字节相同的响应，且不产生第二次事务。 */
    TEST_CHECK(dt_fixture_init(&fixture, true));
    dt_core_device_ready(&fixture, 5u, LOCKER_DOOR_STATE_CLOSED, LOCKER_ITEM_STATE_ABSENT);
    principal = dt_principal(LOCKER_ROLE_USER, 0x70u);
    TEST_CHECK(dt_seed_principal(&fixture, &principal));
    dt_uuid_fill(&id, 0x51u);
    dt_uuid_fill(&tool, 0x22u);
    TEST_CHECK(dc_seed_active(&fixture, LOCKER_OPERATION_KIND_BORROW, LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_WAIT_CONFIRM, 4u,
                              "2025-10-21T00:00:00Z", &id, &tool, &principal.user_id));
    {
        char key[LOCKER_DOMAIN_UUID_TEXT];
        dt_uuid_text(&id, key, sizeof(key));
        TEST_CHECK(dc_patch_doc(&fixture, "operations", key, "\"item_transition_seen\":false",
                                "\"item_transition_seen\":true"));
    }
    {
        locker_command_t command = dc_command(&principal, LOCKER_CORE_OPERATION_CONFIRM,
                                              "{\"operation_revision\":\"4\"}", 0x41u, 0x42u);
        command.resource_id = id;
        TEST_CHECK_STATUS(LOCKER_STATUS_OK, dc_run_command(&fixture, &command, &first));
    }
    commit_before = fixture.repo->commit_calls;
    uint64_t revision_after = fixture.core->revision;
    {
        /* 换一个 request_id 重放：幂等只看幂等键与请求哈希。 */
        locker_command_t command = dc_command(&principal, LOCKER_CORE_OPERATION_CONFIRM,
                                              "{\"operation_revision\":\"4\"}", 0x43u, 0x42u);
        command.resource_id = id;
        TEST_CHECK_STATUS(LOCKER_STATUS_OK, dc_run_command(&fixture, &command, &replay));
    }
    TEST_CHECK_STRING(first.json, replay.json);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(commit_before, (uint64_t)fixture.repo->commit_calls);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(revision_after, fixture.core->revision);
    {
        /* 同一把幂等键换参数（比如换个 operation_revision）-> CONFLICT，
           不能把两种意图当成一次。 */
        locker_command_t command = dc_command(&principal, LOCKER_CORE_OPERATION_CONFIRM,
                                              "{\"operation_revision\":\"9\"}", 0x44u, 0x42u);
        command.resource_id = id;
        TEST_CHECK_STATUS(LOCKER_STATUS_CONFLICT, dc_run_command(&fixture, &command, &conflict));
    }
    dt_fixture_free(&fixture);

    /* 取消：REQUESTED 直接取消，同键重放仍然 OK（状态机单独看会说「已终态」）。 */
    TEST_CHECK(dt_fixture_init(&fixture, true));
    dt_core_device_ready(&fixture, 5u, LOCKER_DOOR_STATE_CLOSED, LOCKER_ITEM_STATE_PRESENT);
    TEST_CHECK(dt_seed_principal(&fixture, &principal));
    TEST_CHECK(dc_seed_active(&fixture, LOCKER_OPERATION_KIND_BORROW, LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REQUESTED, 2u,
                              "2025-10-21T00:00:00Z", &id, &tool, &principal.user_id));
    {
        locker_command_t command = dc_command(&principal, LOCKER_CORE_OPERATION_CANCEL,
                                              "{\"operation_revision\":\"2\",\"reason\":\"changed mind\"}", 0x41u,
                                              0x45u);
        command.resource_id = id;
        TEST_CHECK_STATUS(LOCKER_STATUS_OK, dc_run_command(&fixture, &command, &first));
    }
    commit_before = fixture.repo->commit_calls;
    revision_after = fixture.core->revision;
    {
        locker_command_t command = dc_command(&principal, LOCKER_CORE_OPERATION_CANCEL,
                                              "{\"operation_revision\":\"2\",\"reason\":\"changed mind\"}", 0x46u,
                                              0x45u);
        command.resource_id = id;
        TEST_CHECK_STATUS(LOCKER_STATUS_OK, dc_run_command(&fixture, &command, &replay));
    }
    TEST_CHECK_STRING(first.json, replay.json);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(commit_before, (uint64_t)fixture.repo->commit_calls);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(revision_after, fixture.core->revision);

    /* 核验：最不能重复执行的一条。第一次成功后再发一次同键请求，必须拿到原响应
       而不是 CONFLICT——否则管理员会以为第一次没生效，换个键再来一次。 */
    dt_fixture_free(&fixture);
    TEST_CHECK(dt_fixture_init(&fixture, true));
    dt_core_device_ready(&fixture, 5u, LOCKER_DOOR_STATE_CLOSED, LOCKER_ITEM_STATE_ABSENT);
    principal = dt_principal(LOCKER_ROLE_ADMIN, 0x80u);
    TEST_CHECK(dt_seed_principal(&fixture, &principal));
    TEST_CHECK(dc_seed_active(&fixture, LOCKER_OPERATION_KIND_BORROW, LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REVIEW_REQUIRED, 6u,
                              "2025-10-21T00:00:00Z", &id, &tool, &principal.user_id));
    {
        locker_command_t command = dc_command(&principal, LOCKER_CORE_OPERATION_RESOLVE,
                                              "{\"operation_revision\":\"6\",\"physical_revision\":\"7\","
                                              "\"resolution\":\"COMPLETE_BORROW\",\"reason\":\"operator checked\"}",
                                              0x41u, 0x47u);
        command.resource_id = id;
        TEST_CHECK_STATUS(LOCKER_STATUS_OK, dc_run_command(&fixture, &command, &first));
    }
    commit_before = fixture.repo->commit_calls;
    revision_after = fixture.core->revision;
    {
        locker_command_t command = dc_command(&principal, LOCKER_CORE_OPERATION_RESOLVE,
                                              "{\"operation_revision\":\"6\",\"physical_revision\":\"7\","
                                              "\"resolution\":\"COMPLETE_BORROW\",\"reason\":\"operator checked\"}",
                                              0x48u, 0x47u);
        command.resource_id = id;
        TEST_CHECK_STATUS(LOCKER_STATUS_OK, dc_run_command(&fixture, &command, &replay));
    }
    TEST_CHECK_STRING(first.json, replay.json);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(commit_before, (uint64_t)fixture.repo->commit_calls);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(revision_after, fixture.core->revision);
    dt_fixture_free(&fixture);
}

/* ---------- 柜前证明 + 起动作（arm） ---------- */

{
    dt_fixture_t fixture;
    locker_device_reply_t reply;

    TEST_CHECK(dt_fixture_init(&fixture, true));
    dt_core_device_ready(&fixture, 1u, LOCKER_DOOR_STATE_CLOSED, LOCKER_ITEM_STATE_PRESENT);
    dv_arm_operation(fixture.core, LOCKER_OPERATION_KIND_BORROW,
                     LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_PENDING, 0x50u);

    /* 查询到 PULSE_STARTED：说明动作真的执行过，推进到 ACCESS_ACTIVE。 */
    memset(&reply, 0, sizeof(reply));
    reply.message_type = (uint8_t)(8u | 0x80u);
    reply.body.query_command.result = LOCKER_WIRE_RESULT_OK;
    reply.body.query_command.operation_id = fixture.core->active.id;
    reply.body.query_command.command_state = LOCKER_COMMAND_STATE_PULSE_STARTED;
    domain_reply_apply(fixture.core, &reply);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_ACTIVE, fixture.core->active.state);
    TEST_CHECK_STRING("PULSE_STARTED", fixture.core->active.reason_code);

    /* 查询失败：进复核（结果未知不是未执行，也不是执行成功）。 */
    dt_core_device_ready(&fixture, 1u, LOCKER_DOOR_STATE_CLOSED, LOCKER_ITEM_STATE_PRESENT);
    dv_arm_operation(fixture.core, LOCKER_OPERATION_KIND_BORROW,
                     LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_PENDING, 0x50u);
    memset(&reply, 0, sizeof(reply));
    reply.message_type = (uint8_t)(8u | 0x80u);
    reply.body.query_command.result = LOCKER_WIRE_RESULT_NOT_READY;
    reply.body.query_command.operation_id = fixture.core->active.id;
    domain_reply_apply(fixture.core, &reply);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REVIEW_REQUIRED, fixture.core->active.state);
    TEST_CHECK_STRING("QUERY_UNRESOLVED", fixture.core->active.reason_code);

    /* 操作 id 不符：不动状态。 */
    dt_core_device_ready(&fixture, 1u, LOCKER_DOOR_STATE_CLOSED, LOCKER_ITEM_STATE_PRESENT);
    dv_arm_operation(fixture.core, LOCKER_OPERATION_KIND_BORROW,
                     LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_PENDING, 0x50u);
    memset(&reply, 0, sizeof(reply));
    reply.message_type = (uint8_t)(8u | 0x80u);
    reply.body.query_command.result = LOCKER_WIRE_RESULT_OK;
    reply.body.query_command.command_state = LOCKER_COMMAND_STATE_PULSE_STARTED;
    dt_uuid_fill(&reply.body.query_command.operation_id, 0x99u);
    domain_reply_apply(fixture.core, &reply);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_PENDING, fixture.core->active.state);

    dt_fixture_free(&fixture);
}

/* ---------- ACK 回复 ---------- */

static void test_ack_response(void)
        out->has_command = true;
    }
    const locker_json_node_t *reason = domain_json_member(doc, node, "reason_code");
    out->reason_code[0] = '\0';
    if (reason != NULL && reason->type != LOCKER_JSON_NULL) {
        if (!domain_json_read_text(doc, reason, out->reason_code, sizeof(out->reason_code))) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    if (!domain_json_read_text(doc, domain_json_member(doc, node, "created_at"), out->created_at, sizeof(out->created_a
t))) {
        return false;
    }
    if (!domain_json_read_text(doc, domain_json_member(doc, node, "updated_at"), out->updated_at, sizeof(out->updated_a
t))) {
        return false;
    }
    /*
     * created_ms/updated_ms 是运行时字段，文档里只有墙钟文本；必须在这里还原成
     * 毫秒值，否则重启恢复时 updated_ms 恒为 0，deadline 会退化成「now + TTL」，
     * 把早已过期的申请当成刚创建（domain_core.c:143 与 :484 都直接用它算截止点）。
     */
    if (!locker_domain_time_parse_utc(out->created_at, strlen(out->created_at), &out->created_ms)) {
        return false;
    }
    if (!locker_domain_time_parse_utc(out->updated_at, strlen(out->updated_at), &out->updated_ms)) {
        return false;
    }
    return parse_evidence(doc, domain_json_member(doc, node, "evidence"), out);
}

static bool parse_operation_record(const locker_json_document_t *doc, const locker_json_node_t *root,
                                   domain_operation_t *out)
{
    if (doc == NULL || root == NULL || out == NULL) {
        return false;
    }
    memset(out, 0, sizeof(*out));
    if (!parse_operation_object(doc, domain_json_member(doc, root, "operation"), out)) {
        return false;
    }
    if (!parse_physical(doc, domain_json_member(doc, root, "baseline"), &out->baseline)) {
        return false;
    }
    out->has_baseline = true;
    const locker_json_node_t *loan = domain_json_member(doc, root, "related_loan_id");
    out->has_loan = false;
    if (loan != NULL && loan->type != LOCKER_JSON_NULL) {
        if (!domain_json_read_uuid(doc, loan, &out->related_loan_id)) {
            return false;
        }
        out->has_loan = true;
    }
    const locker_json_node_t *proof = domain_json_member(doc, root, "proof_source");
    out->proof_source = 0u;
    if (proof != NULL && proof->type != LOCKER_JSON_NULL) {
        char text[16];
        size_t length = 0u;
        if (!locker_domain_json_text(doc, proof, text, sizeof(text), &length)) {
            return false;
        }
        if (length == 4u && memcmp(text, "CARD", 4) == 0) {
            out->proof_source = 1u;
        } else if (length == 8u && memcmp(text, "LOCAL_UI", 8) == 0) {
            out->proof_source = 2u;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }
    if (!domain_json_read_bool(doc, domain_json_member(doc, root, "proof_consumed"), &out->proof_consumed)) {
        return false;
    }
    out->used = true;
    return true;
}

bool domain_operation_from_record(const locker_json_document_t *doc, const locker_json_node_t *root,
                                 domain_operation_t *out)
{
    return parse_operation_record(doc, root, out);
}

bool domain_import_cursor_from_record(const locker_json_document_t *doc, const locker_json_node_t *root,
                                      locker_core_t *core)
{
    if (doc == NULL || root == NULL || core == NULL) {
        return false;
    }
    char device_id[25];
    if (!domain_json_read_text(doc, domain_json_member(doc, root, "device_id"), device_id, sizeof(device_id))) {
        return false;
    }
    locker_uuid_t epoch;
    if (!domain_json_read_uuid(doc, domain_json_member(doc, root, "journal_epoch"), &epoch)) {
        return false;
    }
    locker_event_key_t through;
    if (!parse_event_key(doc, domain_json_member(doc, root, "through"), &through)) {
        return false;
    }
    bool continuity = false;
    if (!domain_json_read_bool(doc, domain_json_member(doc, root, "continuity_ok"), &continuity)) {
        return false;
    }
    if (!core->import_cursor_valid) {
        core->import_cursor = through;
        core->import_cursor_valid = true;
        core->import_continuity = continuity;
        core->cursor.valid = true;
        core->cursor.journal_epoch = epoch;
        memcpy(core->cursor.device_id, device_id, sizeof(core->cursor.device_id));
        core->cursor.through = through;
        core->cursor.continuity_ok = continuity;
        core->ack_cursor = through;
        core->ack_cursor_valid = true;
    }
    return true;
}

bool domain_json_read_enum_device_mode(const locker_json_document_t *doc, const locker_json_node_t *node,
                                      uint8_t *out)
{
    char text[16];
    size_t length = 0u;
    if (node == NULL) {
        return false;
    }
    if (!locker_domain_json_text(doc, node, text, sizeof(text), &length)) {
        return false;
    }
    static const char *const k_modes[] = {"OFFLINE", "RECONCILING", "READY", "DEGRADED"};
    for (uint8_t i = 0u; i < 4u; ++i) {
        if (strlen(k_modes[i]) == length && memcmp(k_modes[i], text, length) == 0) {
            *out = i;
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

locker_status_t domain_operation_load(locker_core_t *core, const locker_uuid_t *id, int slot,
                                      domain_operation_t *out)
{
    if (core == NULL || id == NULL || out == NULL || slot < 0 || slot > 1) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    char key[LOCKER_DOMAIN_UUID_TEXT];
    if (!domain_uuid_to_text(id, key, sizeof(key))) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    domain_doc_t doc;
    locker_status_t status = domain_repo_doc(core, slot, "operations", key, &doc);
    if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        return status;
    }
    if (!doc.found || doc.value == NULL) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    if (!parse_operation_record(&doc.doc, doc.value, out)) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_IO_ERROR;
    }
    /* 运行时计时字段不在文档里：用最近一次更新时间重建保守截止点。 */
    if (out->state == LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REQUESTED) {
        out->requested_deadline_ms = out->updated_ms + LOCKER_REQUEST_TTL_MS;
    } else if (out->state == LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_WAIT_CONFIRM) {
        out->wait_confirm_deadline_ms = out->updated_ms + LOCKER_WAIT_CONFIRM_MS;
    }
    return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
}

locker_status_t domain_persist_operation(locker_core_t *core, domain_operation_t *operation)
{
    if (core == NULL || operation == NULL || !operation->used) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    char key[LOCKER_DOMAIN_UUID_TEXT];
    if (!domain_uuid_to_text(&operation->id, key, sizeof(key))) {

| 当前状态 | 输入 | 结果 |
|---|---|---|
| REQUESTED | 有效柜前证明，REQUIRED的PRE_ACCESS相符并持久化 | ACCESS_PENDING，提交唯一command；视觉未通过保持REQUESTED无动作 |
| REQUESTED | 取消或 120 秒过期，且没有动作意图 | CANCELLED，释放占位 |
| ACCESS_PENDING | ACCEPTED ACK | 保持，不能显示门已开 |
| ACCESS_PENDING | PULSE_STARTED 事实 | ACCESS_ACTIVE |
| ACCESS_PENDING | 明确 REJECTED 且证实未接纳/未驱动 | FAILED；底层健康问题不因此消失 |
| ACCESS_ACTIVE | 完整开关门/目标变化；REQUIRED的POST视觉相符且落库 | WAIT_CONFIRM |
| ACCESS_ACTIVE | 完整开关门且始终未变；REQUIRED视觉仍符合原基线 | NO_CHANGE，账本不变；视觉矛盾则核验 |
| WAIT_CONFIRM | 本人确认、当前条件仍符合、无缺口 | COMPLETED |
| WAIT_CONFIRM | 120 秒未确认或物理状态再变 | REVIEW_REQUIRED |
| 可能已执行的任意状态 | 取消、结果未知、冲突、服务/MCU 重启 | REVIEW_REQUIRED，保持占位 |
| REVIEW_REQUIRED | 管理员现场核验且门关/物品明确 | COMPLETED 或 NO_CHANGE，保留理由与人工标识 |

从脉冲事实起 30 秒未见开门、开门后 120 秒未关门，进入核验。UI 重启不改变操作。仅 UI 显示异常但服务继续接收完整物理事实时，可恢复显示后继续当前操作；不能重发开锁。

REQ 创建、确认、取消、管理员核验都做服务端鉴权和乐观版本检查。确认只能在 WAIT_CONFIRM，不接受提前确认。已结束的资源不再改变结果；客户端重试同一意图必须保留同一幂等键。

### 管理员核验

只支持 COMPLETE_BORROW、COMPLETE_RETURN、NO_CHANGE，必须匹配原操作类型/原用户、当前门关闭和明确物品状态，并提供原因。COMPLETE_BORROW要求离位并创建原用户借用；COMPLETE_RET
URN要求在位并关闭原借用；NO_CHANGE要求回到原基线（借出为在位、归还为离位），账本不变。不能转给第三人、覆盖原事件或在传感器UNKNOWN时强制完成。证据缺口经现场核验可以显式承认，但永远保留缺口与人工处理事实。

#include "domain_internal.h"

/* 操作工作副本与持久化投影。
 *
 * 状态机的事实来源是 docs/stages/stage-00-architecture.md:106-128 的状态表；
 * 本文件只负责「把内存里的操作写成 schema 认可的 OperationRecord」，以及状态
 * 优先级与允许动作的推导，不做任何设备 IO。 */

void domain_operation_reset(domain_operation_t *operation)
{
    if (operation == NULL) {
        return;
    }
    memset(operation, 0, sizeof(*operation));
}

void domain_operation_allowed_actions(const domain_operation_t *operation, uint8_t *out, uint32_t *count)
{
    if (operation == NULL || out == NULL || count == NULL) {
        return;
    }
    uint32_t used = 0u;
    switch (operation->state) {
    case LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REQUESTED:
        out[used++] = LOCKER_ALLOWED_ACTION_PRESENT_AT_CABINET;
        out[used++] = LOCKER_ALLOWED_ACTION_WAIT;
        out[used++] = LOCKER_ALLOWED_ACTION_CANCEL;
        break;
    case LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_PENDING:
        /* 命令可能已被接纳：取消会变成结果未知，因此只给等待。 */
        out[used++] = LOCKER_ALLOWED_ACTION_WAIT;
        break;
    case LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_ACTIVE:
        out[used++] = LOCKER_ALLOWED_ACTION_WAIT;
        break;
    case LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_WAIT_CONFIRM:
        out[used++] = LOCKER_ALLOWED_ACTION_CONFIRM;
        out[used++] = LOCKER_ALLOWED_ACTION_WAIT;
        break;
    case LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REVIEW_REQUIRED:
        out[used++] = LOCKER_ALLOWED_ACTION_ADMIN_REVIEW;
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
    *count = used;
}

void domain_operation_touch(locker_core_t *core, domain_operation_t *operation)
{
    if (core == NULL || operation == NULL) {
        return;
    }
    uint64_t now = domain_now(core);
    char text[LOCKER_DOMAIN_TIMESTAMP_TEXT];
    uint64_t wall = 0u;
    if (locker_clock_port_wall_utc_ms(&core->clock, &wall) != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        wall = 0u;
    }
    if (!locker_domain_time_format_utc(wall, text, sizeof(text))) {
        text[0] = '\0';
    }
    if (operation->created_at[0] == '\0') {
        domain_text_copy(operation->created_at, sizeof(operation->created_at), text);
        operation->created_ms = now;
    }
    domain_text_copy(operation->updated_at, sizeof(operation->updated_at), text);
    operation->updated_ms = now;
    operation->revision += 1u;
}

void domain_operation_add_source_event(domain_operation_t *operation, const locker_event_key_t *key)
{
    if (operation == NULL || key == NULL) {
        return;
    }
    for (uint32_t i = 0u; i < operation->source_event_count; ++i) {
        const locker_event_key_t *existing = &operation->source_events[i];
        if (locker_uuid_equal(&existing->journal_epoch, &key->journal_epoch) &&
            domain_text_equal(existing->device_id, key->device_id) && existing->boot_id == key->boot_id &&
            existing->event_seq == key->event_seq) {
            return;
        }
    }
    if (operation->source_event_count >= LOCKER_DOMAIN_SOURCE_EVENTS) {
        /* 证据超出上限：保留「历史不完整」事实，绝不静默截断成完整。 */
        operation->history_complete = false;
        return;
    }
    operation->source_events[operation->source_event_count++] = *key;
}

void domain_operation_capture_physical(domain_operation_t *operation, const domain_physical_t *physical)
{
    if (operation == NULL || physical == NULL || !physical->valid) {
        return;
    }
    operation->has_physical = true;
    operation->physical = *physical;
}

locker_status_t domain_operation_persist(locker_core_t *core, domain_change_t *change,
                                         const domain_operation_t *operation)
{
    if (core == NULL || change == NULL || operation == NULL) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    char key[LOCKER_DOMAIN_UUID_TEXT];
    if (!domain_uuid_to_text(&operation->id, key, sizeof(key))) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    if (!domain_change_put_begin(change, "operations", key)) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NO_SPACE;
    }
    return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
}

locker_status_t locker_repo_commit_change(locker_repo_t *repo,
                                                                   const locker_change_t *change)
{
    if (repo == NULL || change == NULL) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    repo->commit_calls += 1u;
    if (change->length + 1u < sizeof(repo->last_change)) {
        memcpy(repo->last_change, change->json, change->length);
        repo->last_change[change->length] = '\0';
        repo->last_change_length = change->length;
    }
    if (repo->commit_status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        return repo->commit_status;
    }
    return dt_repo_apply_change(repo, change->json, change->length);
}

    locker_event_key_t *committed_cursor)

{
    domain_job_t job;
    bool drained = false;
    while (locker_bounded_queue_try_pop(&core->queue, &job) == LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        drained = true;
        if (job.kind == DOMAIN_JOB_STOP) {
            if (job.completion != UINT32_MAX) {
                domain_completion_finish(core, job.completion, LOCKER_STATUS_OK, NULL, 0u, core->revision);
            }
            return;
        }
        domain_handle_job(core, &job);
    }
    /* 设备环：事件与响应都按值复制进来，只在这里出队应用。 */
    domain_event_slot_t event;
    while (locker_bounded_queue_try_pop(&core->event_ring, &event) == LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        domain_handle_device_event(core, &event.event, event.completion);
        drained = true;
    }
    domain_reply_slot_t reply;
    while (locker_bounded_queue_try_pop(&core->reply_ring, &reply) == LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        domain_handle_device_reply(core, &reply.reply, reply.completion);
        drained = true;
    }
    (void)drained;
    /* 真实循环进展：只有完成一次出队+泵才推进，不能靠定时中断自增。 */
    core_tick_internal(core, core_clock_now(core));
}

static void *core_thread_main(void *argument)
{
    locker_core_t *core = (locker_core_t *)argument;
    core->now_ms = core_clock_now(core);
    locker_status_t status = domain_recover(core);
    core->recovery_status = status;
    if (status == LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        core->phase = DOMAIN_PHASE_READY;
    } else {
        /* 恢复失败不清库、不猜状态：保持 RECONCILING，全部写命令回 NOT_READY。 */
        core->phase = DOMAIN_PHASE_RECOVERING;
    }
    domain_progress_publish(core);

    for (;;) {
        core_run_once(core);
        if (stop_requested(core)) {
            break;
        }
        pthread_mutex_lock(&core->mutex);
        if (locker_bounded_queue_size(&core->queue) == 0u && !stop_requested(core)) {
            struct timespec wait;
            clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &wait);
            wait.tv_nsec += (long)DOMAIN_IDLE_WAIT_MS * 1000000L;
            if (wait.tv_nsec >= 1000000000L) {
                wait.tv_sec += 1;
                wait.tv_nsec -= 1000000000L;
            }
            (void)pthread_cond_timedwait(&core->cond, &core->mutex, &wait);
        }
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&core->mutex);
    }

    drain_queue(core);
    domain_device_drain(core);
    domain_phase_t phase = DOMAIN_PHASE_STOPPED;
    core->phase = phase;
    atomic_store_explicit(&core->thread_exited, true, memory_order_release);
    return NULL;
}

/* ---------- 生命周期 ---------- */

static void core_free(locker_core_t *core)
{
    if (core == NULL) {
}

locker_status_t locker_core_get_progress(locker_core_t *core, locker_progress_t *result)
{
    if (core == NULL || result == NULL) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    memset(result, 0, sizeof(*result));
    pthread_mutex_lock(&core->progress_mutex);
    bool valid = core->progress_valid;
    if (valid) {
        *result = core->progress;
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&core->progress_mutex);
    if (!valid) {
        /* 没有真实循环样本时保持未知，绝不合成一个自增计数（契约 :337）。 */
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
}

/* ---------- 本机私有入口 ---------- */

locker_status_t locker_local_presence_submit(locker_core_t *core, const locker_peer_t *peer,
                                             const locker_presence_t *presence)
        return;
    }
    pthread_mutex_lock(&core->completion_mutex);
    domain_completion_t *completion = &core->completions[slot];
    if (completion->used) {
        free(completion->json);
        completion->json = NULL;
        completion->length = 0u;
        completion->final_status = status;
        completion->final_ready = true;
        completion->expires_ms = domain_now(core) + core->completion_retention_ms;
        pthread_cond_broadcast(&core->completion_cond);
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&core->completion_mutex);
}

static void completion_deadline(uint32_t timeout_ms, struct timespec *out)
{
    /* 等待上限由契约固定：wait_result 最多 1000 ms，超时不取消。 */
    uint32_t capped = timeout_ms > LOCKER_CORE_RESULT_WAIT_MS ? LOCKER_CORE_RESULT_WAIT_MS : timeout_ms;
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, out);
    out->tv_sec += (time_t)(capped / 1000u);
    out->tv_nsec += (long)(capped % 1000u) * 1000000L;
    if (out->tv_nsec >= 1000000000L) {
        out->tv_sec += 1;
        out->tv_nsec -= 1000000000L;
    }
}

locker_status_t domain_completion_wait_accept(locker_core_t *core, uint32_t slot, uint32_t timeout_ms,
                                              locker_status_t *out_status, locker_uuid_t *out_operation,
                                              bool *out_has_operation)
{
    if (core == NULL || slot >= LOCKER_DOMAIN_COMPLETION_SLOTS || out_status == NULL) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    struct timespec deadline;
    completion_deadline(timeout_ms, &deadline);
    pthread_mutex_lock(&core->completion_mutex);
    domain_completion_t *completion = &core->completions[slot];
    while (completion->used && !completion->accept_ready) {
        int rc = pthread_cond_timedwait(&core->completion_cond, &core->completion_mutex, &deadline);
        if (rc != 0) {
            break;
        }
    }
    bool ready = completion->used && completion->accept_ready;
    locker_status_t status = LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
    if (ready) {
        *out_status = completion->accept_status;
        if (out_has_operation != NULL) {
            *out_has_operation = completion->has_operation;
        }
        if (out_operation != NULL) {
            *out_operation = completion->operation_id;
        }
    } else {
        status = LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&core->completion_mutex);
    return status;
}

locker_status_t domain_completion_wait_final(locker_core_t *core, uint32_t slot, uint32_t timeout_ms,
                                             locker_query_result_t *out_result)
{
    if (core == NULL || slot >= LOCKER_DOMAIN_COMPLETION_SLOTS || out_result == NULL) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    struct timespec deadline;
    completion_deadline(timeout_ms, &deadline);
    pthread_mutex_lock(&core->completion_mutex);
    domain_completion_t *completion = &core->completions[slot];
    while (completion->used && !completion->final_ready) {
        int rc = pthread_cond_timedwait(&core->completion_cond, &core->completion_mutex, &deadline);
        if (rc != 0) {
            break;
        }
    }
    locker_status_t status = LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    if (completion->used && completion->final_ready) {
        status = LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
        out_result->status = completion->final_status;
        out_result->snapshot_revision = completion->revision;
        out_result->length = 0u;
        out_result->json[0] = '\0';
        if (completion->json != NULL) {
            if (completion->length + 1u > sizeof(out_result->json)) {
                status = LOCKER_STATUS_NO_SPACE;
            } else {
                memcpy(out_result->json, completion->json, completion->length + 1u);
                out_result->length = completion->length;
            }
        }
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&core->completion_mutex);
    return status;
}

uint32_t domain_completion_find(locker_core_t *core, const locker_uuid_t *token)
{
    if (core == NULL || token == NULL) {
        return UINT32_MAX;
    }
    uint32_t found = UINT32_MAX;
    pthread_mutex_lock(&core->completion_mutex);
    for (uint32_t i = 0u; i < LOCKER_DOMAIN_COMPLETION_SLOTS; ++i) {
        if (core->completions[i].used && locker_uuid_equal(&core->completions[i].token, token)) {
            found = i;
            break;
        }
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&core->completion_mutex);
    return found;
}

void domain_completion_set_principal(locker_core_t *core, uint32_t slot, const locker_principal_t *principal)
{
    if (core == NULL || slot >= LOCKER_DOMAIN_COMPLETION_SLOTS || principal == NULL) {
        return;
    }
    pthread_mutex_lock(&core->completion_mutex);
    domain_completion_t *completion = &core->completions[slot];
    if (completion->used) {
        completion->principal = *principal;
        completion->has_principal = true;
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&core->completion_mutex);
}

bool domain_completion_take_principal(locker_core_t *core, uint32_t slot, locker_principal_t *out)
{
    if (core == NULL || slot >= LOCKER_DOMAIN_COMPLETION_SLOTS || out == NULL) {
        return false;
    }
    bool ok = false;
    pthread_mutex_lock(&core->completion_mutex);
    domain_completion_t *completion = &core->completions[slot];
    if (completion->used && completion->has_principal) {
        *out = completion->principal;
        ok = true;
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&core->completion_mutex);
    return ok;
}

void domain_completion_reap(locker_core_t *core, uint64_t now_ms)
{
    if (core == NULL) {
        return;
    }
    pthread_mutex_lock(&core->completion_mutex);
    for (uint32_t i = 0u; i < LOCKER_DOMAIN_COMPLETION_SLOTS; ++i) {
        domain_completion_t *completion = &core->completions[i];
        if (!completion->used || !completion->final_ready) {
            continue;
        }
        /* 只回收已经交付过终态的槽：未取走的终态保留到保留期结束，避免调用方
           永远等不到结果；保留期由 core->completion_retention_ms 决定。 */
        if (completion->expires_ms != 0u && now_ms >= completion->expires_ms) {
            free(completion->json);
            memset(completion, 0, sizeof(*completion));
        }
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&core->completion_mutex);
}

bool domain_completion_take_json(locker_core_t *core, uint32_t slot, char *out, size_t out_size,
                                 size_t *out_length, uint64_t *out_revision)
{
    if (core == NULL || slot >= LOCKER_DOMAIN_COMPLETION_SLOTS || out == NULL || out_size == 0u) {
        return false;
    }
    bool ok = false;
    pthread_mutex_lock(&core->completion_mutex);
    domain_completion_t *completion = &core->completions[slot];
    if (completion->used && completion->final_ready && completion->json != NULL &&
        completion->length + 1u <= out_size) {
        memcpy(out, completion->json, completion->length + 1u);
        if (out_length != NULL) {
            *out_length = completion->length;
        }
        if (out_revision != NULL) {
            *out_revision = completion->revision;
        }
        ok = true;
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&core->completion_mutex);
    return ok;
}
# 模块所有权与 AI 修改边界

执行先读[阶段导航](../stages/README.md)和[机器阶段索引](../../config/development/stage-plan.json)。45张卡默认一张一张开发，具体路径限制在该卡；下表是模块所有权，不授权一
次实现整个目录。逐接口归属见[追踪表](traceability.md)。

状态：阶段 0 设计基线的模块交接约定，尚无业务实现。当前执行语义以[阶段 0 主规格](../stages/stage-00-architecture.md)及[机器真源](../../contracts/contract-source
.json)为准。专家评审是设计背景，不是另一套实现依据。

## 1. 先读契约，再划分实现

阅读顺序：阶段 0 → [OpenAPI](../../contracts/openapi.json) → [C 接口声明](../../contracts/include/locker_contracts.h) → [内部接口](int
ernal-interfaces.md) → [UART-v1](uart-v1.md) → [阶段 1 任务卡](../stages/stage-01-task-cards.md)。

`contracts/contract-source.json` 是跨端枚举、错误、字段、消息编号和端口的唯一机器真源；`scripts/build_contracts.py` 生成公开产物。功能开发 AI 不直接编辑生成文件，不复制另一
套相似枚举，不为通过自身测试擅自修改契约。发现缺项或矛盾时，提交“位置、冲突、受影响调用方、建议”的变更说明，由架构负责人统一修改真源、重新生成并验证，再继续依赖该变更的实现。

新增模块私有字段不得绕过契约对外输出。私有结构体可用于实现，但不能直接作为公共 JSON 或串口字节。

## 2. 固定目录和责任

下表中的目录是后续开发的修改边界；当前阶段不要求预建空实现。

| 目录/入口 | 所有者角色 | 内容与权限 | 禁止事项 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `contracts/`、`scripts/build_contracts.py`、`scripts/check_contracts.py` | 架构/契约负责人 | 机器真源、生成器、黄金样例、一致性校验 | 普通业务卡手改生成文件
或放宽断言 |
| `host/common/` | 基础设施负责人 | 有界队列、单调时钟、UUID/u64 校验、受限日志、内存生命周期规则 | 放业务决策、数据库或锁操作 |
| `host/domain/` | 业务核心负责人 | 借还状态机、权限决策、占位、确认、核验、重连状态、outbox 调度意图 | 包含 CivetWeb/SQLite/HAL 头、调用网络/串口阻塞 IO、让客户端指定真实 acto
r |
| `host/storage/` | SQLite 负责人 | Repository 适配、DDL/迁移、事务、幂等记录、事件/游标和 outbox | 在 HTTP 线程直接调用；把动作发出放进 SQLite 回调 |
| `host/device/` | 设备协议负责人 | DevicePort、UART-v1 编解码、会话/命令结果、事件导入入口；阶段 1 只接模拟 socket | 改业务账本、重发已可能发送的 EXECUTE、把 CRC 当鉴权 
|
| `host/http/` | 网络接口负责人 | CivetWeb HTTPS/WS、body/分页限制、会话提取、领域命令适配 | 直接 SQL、GPIO、公共 presence/模拟注入/任意开锁接口 |
| `host/ipc/` | 本机接口负责人 | `service.sock` 的 SO_PEERCRED 与权限检查、当前用户会话和到柜证明适配 | 仅相信 JSON 的 `source=local`、仅凭本机来源冒充借用人 |
| `host/runtime/` | 集成负责人 | `locker-service` 入口、配置、线程装配、退出清理、真实 Core 进度 | 把所有权不明的共享可变状态塞到全局变量；自动重放旧 outbox |
| `host/cli/` | 集成/安全负责人 | `locker-admin` 本机初始化；阶段 1 测试客户端工具 | 默认账号密码、网络注册、命令行明文密码、无审计状态覆盖 |
| `host/simulator/` | 模拟器负责人 | 独立 `locker-device-sim`、设备模型、私有故障注入、独立模拟持久化 | 连接真实锁、访问业务数据库、向公网暴露控制入口、宣称 littlefs 已验证 |
| `tests/unit/`、`tests/contract/`、`tests/integration/`、`tests/fixtures/` | 对应模块 + 验证负责人 | 正式回归测试、跨模块场景和公开/内部契约断言 | 用只反射
实现的断言代替验收；留下真实凭据；替换生产错误语义 |
| `deployment/host/` | 集成负责人 | 无凭据配置样例、主机模拟启动说明和依赖锁定清单 | 把模拟启动脚本当真机部署；隐藏绑定到所有网卡 |
| `CMakeLists.txt`、根级构建配置 | 集成负责人 | 唯一主机构建入口；模块卡通过局部 CMake 文件接入 | 多个 AI 同时改根构建导致入口分裂 |
| `docs/evidence/stage-01/` | 验证负责人 | 正式阶段报告、测试条件、证据索引和已知限制 | 把模拟/静态结果描述为实物通过 |
| `firmware/`、`app/`、`terminal-ui/` | 后续阶段负责人 | MCU、Android、LVGL 实现，当前阶段保留目录名 | 阶段 1 扩写真实固件、完整 App 或 GUI |
| `host/supervisor/` | 阶段 4 监督负责人 | 固定 UI 进程监督与限次恢复 | 首版自动重启 service、硬复位 RK 或控制锁 |
| `app/` | S3客户端负责人 | Android配对/凭据/非敏感意图/页面；按各卡子目录拆分 | 持久化密码、任意URL、直连MCU、第二账本 |
| `terminal-ui/` | S3触摸端负责人 | 单UI线程、异步IPC、实际回路进度 | 在IPC线程改LVGL、重启重放presence、保留旧token |
| `deployment/target/`、`deployment/maintenance/`、`deployment/releases/` | S2首用/S5部署负责人 | 真实目标部署、受限维护和固定发布清单 | 裸复制营业DB、回
退水位/额度、远程shell/OTA扩项 |
| `config/development/`、`scripts/build_stage_docs.py`、`scripts/check_stage_plan.py` | 架构/集成负责人 | 单卡依赖/状态、接口归属与派生追踪表 | 填
假DONE、并行共改状态、手改生成追踪表 |
| `host/vision-worker/` | S2-11/12/13视觉负责人 | V4L2/RKNN/私有worker服务，逐卡限目录 | 访问UART/账本、公网classify、网络图片路径 |
| `host/vision/` | S2-13适配负责人 | 私有IPC/有界队列/sink/超时/实例 | Core阻塞NPU、写SQL、以分类授权锁 |
| `tools/vision/`、`deployment/vision/`、`config/vision/` | S2-09..12模型/集成负责人 | 离线导出/评估、版本化制品、策略/profile | 覆盖原模型、虚报RKNN/相
机/预算 |

并行 AI 只能改分配目录及其模块测试，不共改根 CMake、契约、同一份 DDL 或跨模块头文件。需要扩展共享声明时由集成/契约负责人先提交统一版本，再解除依赖任务阻塞；不得双方各造一份兼容层。

## 3. 运行时单写者和数据流

- `locker-service` 的 Core 线程拥有借还状态和唯一 SQLite 写连接。仓储调用只发生在该线程；同步事务必须有错误返回，提交失败不发动作。
- HTTP/IPC 工作线程、设备接收线程把复制后的命令/事实放入有界队列，不保留请求栈或 CivetWeb 缓冲区指针。排队成功与持久化完成分开发回。
- 设备事实由 Core 幂等导入；只有事件与游标同事务提交，才能确认 MCU 日志水位。慢 WS 客户端不能阻塞 Core。
- Runtime仅经core_get_progress读源副本，经core_apply_supervisor_health提交监督观察；Core唯一更新健康投影。Progress采样原时刻随副本保留，不能搬运时刷新或重复累计年龄。
- 业务 outbox 表示“需要发送的持久意图”，不意味着重启后自动重发。可能发送过的动作进入查询/核对；服务新实例必须建立新 session，旧动作不得迁移。
- 模拟器独占自己的设备模型和模拟持久化文件，业务服务与模拟器不得共享同一个 SQLite 文件或写连接。
- 模拟器通过私有 `AF_UNIX SOCK_STREAM` 向 DevicePort 提供真实 UART-v1 字节流；测试控制走另一条权限 `0600` 的本地控制入口，不能混入公共 HTTPS。这样可以测试半帧/连续帧等逻辑，仍不
能证明真实 DMA、Flash 或电气行为。

SQLite DDL 只有 `host/storage/schema-v1.sql` 一处执行真源。阶段 1 的建表职责属于仓储卡，状态语义必须完全来自主规格和契约；数据库迁移版本单调递增，不自动删库重建来绕过不兼容。外部 u64 可能超
过 SQLite 有符号整数范围，存储适配应以经过边界校验的十进制文本保存；排序/水位比较按数值处理，不能直接按普通文本字典序比较。

## 4. 监督与物理控制的边界

首版自动恢复对象只有 `locker-ui`。`locker-service` 失联由独立 MCU 记录，人工重启服务后必须先核对，不能自动重放开锁。阶段 1 不实现监督守护进程，只保留契约端口和真实业务进度观测，相关自动恢复验收到阶段 
4。

MCU Control 是锁输出唯一入口；PeripheralStorage 是 littlefs/SPI 唯一所有者；Communication 只能提交消息，Health 不能代替业务授权。阶段 1 的模拟器实现这些角色的逻辑结果，不
允许被当成 HAL/BSP 的替代实现交付真机。

到柜证明、用户确认和物理事实是三种输入：App 不能伪造到柜证明，管理员不能上传原始传感器事件，实时读卡和历史补传必须区分。管理员核验保留人工理由及证据缺口，不改变原始设备事件。

+/* observed_age_ms 是**公开**的新鲜度声明，必须由 Core 按自己的单调时钟算出来：
+   写死 0 会让离线很久的缓存快照在投影里看起来像刚采到的，管理员据此改账就等于
+   用过期物理事实冒充当前事实（审计 R1-B-12）。未采样（observed_mono_ms==0 或
+   now 早于采样点）时不声称新鲜，写 uint32 上限表示「未知」，与
+   ProgressReport.observed_age_ms 的既有语义一致。 */
+static uint32_t physical_observed_age_ms(const domain_physical_t *physical, uint64_t now_ms)
+{
+    if (physical->observed_mono_ms == 0u || now_ms < physical->observed_mono_ms) {
+        return UINT32_MAX;
+    }
+    uint64_t age = now_ms - physical->observed_mono_ms;
+    if (age > (uint64_t)UINT32_MAX) {
+        return UINT32_MAX;
+    }
+    return (uint32_t)age;
+}

#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L

/*
 * S1-04 业务核心：有效借用投影（loans）。
 *
 * 借还账本只有 Core 线程写（module-ownership.md:49）。两条约束来自真源：
 *   - loans 以 loan.id 为 key；归还关闭原记录，禁止覆盖借用者（x-rules 第 7 条）。
 *   - 操作终结与账本变更必须都落库，任何一步失败都不能宣称借还成功。
 *
 * 本文件只处理 loans 集合与 core->loan_* 内存副本；operations 的
 * related_loan_id 由 domain_loan_prepare 在终结事务之前写入。
 *
 * **终结与账本必须同一个事务**：先 `domain_loan_check` 写条件、再
 * `domain_loan_apply` 写变更，两者都挂在调用方的 change 上，由调用方连同
 * 操作状态一起提交。分两次提交会在中途失败时留下「操作 COMPLETED 但 loans
 * 没动」的半截状态——借出侧账上说已借出却没有有效借用、归还侧账上已完成但
 * 借用仍 open，正是 x-rules 第 7 条与
 * `docs/architecture/reviews/interface-expert.md:29` 要求排除的情形。
 *
 * RepositoryChange 的 checks 段必须在 mutations 段之前一次性写完
 * （见 domain_change.c 的 domain_change_check 约束），所以这里严格分成
 * check / apply 两个阶段，不能合成一次调用。
 */

#include "domain_internal.h"

#include <string.h>

/* 借出时先分配 loan_id 并挂到 operation 上，让 OperationRecord.related_loan_id
   与 loans 记录在同一个语义步里出现。 */
locker_status_t domain_loan_prepare(locker_core_t *core, domain_operation_t *operation)
{
    if (core == NULL || operation == NULL || !operation->used) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    if (operation->kind == LOCKER_OPERATION_KIND_BORROW) {
        if (core->loan_open) {
            /* 单柜格：已有有效借用时不允许再借。 */
            return LOCKER_STATUS_CONFLICT;
        }
        locker_uuid_t loan_id;
        if (locker_clock_port_new_uuid(&core->clock, &loan_id) != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
            return LOCKER_STATUS_IO_ERROR;
        }
        operation->has_loan = true;
        operation->related_loan_id = loan_id;
        return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
    }
    if (operation->kind == LOCKER_OPERATION_KIND_RETURN) {
        if (!core->loan_open) {
            return LOCKER_STATUS_CONFLICT;
        }
        /* 归还只能关闭自己的那条借用记录。 */
        if (!locker_uuid_equal(&core->loan_borrower_id, &operation->actor_id)) {
            return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
        }
        operation->has_loan = true;

static locker_status_t device_on_frame(void *user, const locker_wire_frame_t *frame)
{
    locker_device_t *device = (locker_device_t *)user;
    locker_device_reply_t reply;
    locker_device_event_t event;
    locker_wire_response_union_t response;
    locker_wire_event96_t record;
    locker_uuid_t event_session;
    locker_uuid_t event_epoch;
    pending_request_t pending;
    uint16_t decoded;
    bool matched;
    uint8_t request_type;

    if (frame->type == LOCKER_WIRE_DEVICE_EVENT) {
        decoded = locker_wire_decode_device_event(frame, &event_session, &event_epoch, &record);
        if (decoded != LOCKER_WIRE_RESULT_OK) {
            return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
        }
        pthread_mutex_lock(&device->lock);
        /* Events for another session are stale and must not be applied. */
        if (!device->hello_complete || device->reconcile_required ||
            !locker_uuid_equal(&event_session, &device->session_id) ||
            !locker_uuid_equal(&event_epoch, &device->journal_epoch)) {
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&device->lock);
            return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
        }
        memset(&event, 0, sizeof(event));
        snprintf(event.key.device_id, sizeof(event.key.device_id), "%s",
                 device->config.expected_device_id);
        copy_uuid(&event.key.journal_epoch, &event_epoch);
        event.key.boot_id = record.boot_id;
        event.key.event_seq = record.event_seq;
        event.mono_ms = record.mono_ms;
        event.physical_revision = record.physical_revision;
        event.detail = record.detail;
        event.event_type = record.event_type;
        event.reason = record.reason;
        event.flags = record.flags;
        copy_uuid(&event.operation_id, &record.operation_id);
        copy_uuid(&event.command_id, &record.command_id);
        event.door = (locker_door_state_t)record.door;
        event.item = (locker_item_state_t)record.item;
        event.supply_mv = record.supply_mv;
        event.card_uid_len = record.card_uid_len;
        memcpy(event.card_uid, record.card_uid, sizeof(event.card_uid));
        event.live_transport = true;
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&device->lock);
        if (device->sink_bound && device->sink.event != NULL) {
            return device_sink_result(device, device->sink.event(device->sink.user, &event));
        }
        return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
    }

    if ((frame->type & LOCKER_WIRE_RESPONSE_FLAG) == 0u) {
        /* Device-to-host frames are responses or events only. */
        return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
    }
    request_type = (uint8_t)(frame->type & ~LOCKER_WIRE_RESPONSE_FLAG);

    reply_reset(&reply);
    decoded = locker_wire_decode_response(frame, &response);
    if (decoded != LOCKER_WIRE_RESULT_OK) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
    }

    pthread_mutex_lock(&device->lock);
    pending_expire(device);
    matched = !device->reconcile_required &&
              pending_take(device, frame->request_no, request_type, &response.hello.session_id, &pending);
    if (matched && request_type == 1u && response.hello.result == 0u &&
        !device_identity_matches(device, &response.hello)) {
        locker_uuid_t response_session = response.hello.session_id;

    started_ms = transport_real_monotonic_ms();
    deadline_ms = started_ms + (uint64_t)timeout_ms;
    for (;;) {
        locker_status_t status = transport_try_connect(transport, deadline_ms);
        struct timespec pause;
        uint64_t now_ms;

        if (status == LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
            return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
        }
        if (status == LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID) {
            return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
        }
        now_ms = transport_real_monotonic_ms();
        if (now_ms >= deadline_ms) {
            return LOCKER_STATUS_IO_ERROR;
        }
        pause.tv_sec = 0;
        pause.tv_nsec = (long)TRANSPORT_CONNECT_RETRY_MS * 1000000L;
        (void)nanosleep(&pause, NULL);
    }
}

locker_status_t locker_device_transport_write(locker_device_transport_t *transport,
                                              const uint8_t *data, size_t length)
{
    size_t written = 0u;
    uint64_t deadline_ms = transport_real_monotonic_ms() + 1000u;

    if (!locker_device_transport_connected(transport)) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    if (data == NULL && length != 0u) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    while (written < length) {
        struct pollfd writable;
        uint64_t now_ms = transport_real_monotonic_ms();
        int ready;
        ssize_t result;
        if (now_ms >= deadline_ms) {
            transport->connected = false;
            return LOCKER_STATUS_IO_ERROR;
        }
        writable.fd = transport->fd;
        writable.events = POLLOUT;
        writable.revents = 0;
        ready = poll(&writable, 1u, (int)(deadline_ms - now_ms));
        if (ready < 0 && errno == EINTR) {
            continue;
        }
        if (ready <= 0 || (writable.revents & (POLLERR | POLLHUP | POLLNVAL)) != 0) {
            transport->connected = false;
            return LOCKER_STATUS_IO_ERROR;
        }
        result = send(transport->fd, &data[written], length - written,
                      MSG_NOSIGNAL | MSG_DONTWAIT);
        if (result < 0) {
            if (errno == EINTR || errno == EAGAIN || errno == EWOULDBLOCK) {
                continue;
            }
            transport->connected = false;
            return LOCKER_STATUS_IO_ERROR;
        }
        if (result == 0) {
            transport->connected = false;
            return LOCKER_STATUS_IO_ERROR;
        }
        written += (size_t)result;
    }
    return LOCKER_STATUS_OK;
}

int locker_device_transport_read(locker_device_transport_t *transport, uint8_t *buffer,
                                 size_t capacity, int timeout_ms)
{
    struct timeval timeout;
    ssize_t result;

    if (!locker_device_transport_connected(transport) || buffer == NULL || capacity == 0u) {
        return -1;
    }
    timeout.tv_sec = timeout_ms / 1000;
    timeout.tv_usec = (timeout_ms % 1000) * 1000;
    (void)setsockopt(transport->fd, SOL_SOCKET, SO_RCVTIMEO, &timeout, (socklen_t)sizeof(timeout));
    result = recv(transport->fd, buffer, capacity, 0);
    if (result == 0) {
        transport->connected = false;
        return -1;
    }
    if (result < 0) {
        if (errno == EAGAIN || errno == EWOULDBLOCK || errno == EINTR) {
            return 0;
        }
        transport->connected = false;
        return -1;
    }
    return (int)result;
}
    }
    if (request_no == 0u) {
        request_no = pending_next_request_no(device);
    }
    if (request_no == 0u || device->request_numbers_exhausted) {
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&device->lock);
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&device->write_lock);
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    status = locker_wire_encode_request(type, request_no, request, frame, sizeof(frame),
                                        &frame_length);
    if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK ||
        !pending_add(device, request_no, type, session, started_ms)) {
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&device->lock);
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&device->write_lock);
        return status == LOCKER_STATUS_OK ? LOCKER_STATUS_BUSY : LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    if (request_no > device->next_request_no) {
        device->next_request_no = request_no;
    }
    if (request_no == UINT32_MAX) {
        device->request_numbers_exhausted = true;
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&device->lock);
    status = locker_device_transport_write(&device->transport, frame, frame_length);
    if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        pthread_mutex_lock(&device->lock);
        device->reconcile_required = true;
        device->hello_complete = false;
        pending_clear(device);
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&device->lock);
        locker_device_transport_shutdown(&device->transport);
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&device->write_lock);
    if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_IO_ERROR;

# S1-04 round-1 审计 runner（必须保存为 UTF-8 BOM，否则中文路径按 ANSI 解码）
# 用法: powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File audit-runner.ps1
# 不能用 $ErrorActionPreference='Stop'：codex 把版本横幅写到 stderr，PowerShell 会把它当
# NativeCommandError 抛出，整轮在启动瞬间中止。
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
$root = '%REPO%'
$ev   = Join-Path $root 'docs/evidence/stage-01/S1-04'
$in   = Join-Path $ev 'audit-prompt.txt'
$out  = Join-Path $ev 'audit-codex-output.txt'
$log  = Join-Path $ev 'audit-run.log'
Set-Location $root
$start = Get-Date
Get-Content -Raw -Encoding UTF8 $in | codex exec --skip-git-repo-check -s danger-full-access - *> $out
$code = $LASTEXITCODE
$end  = Get-Date
"EXIT=$code" | Out-File -FilePath $log -Encoding utf8
"START=$($start.ToString('s'))" | Out-File -FilePath $log -Encoding utf8 -Append
"END=$($end.ToString('s'))" | Out-File -FilePath $log -Encoding utf8 -Append
"MINUTES=$([math]::Round(($end-$start).TotalMinutes,1))" | Out-File -FilePath $log -Encoding utf8 -Append

static void test_borrow_flow(void)
{
    it_env_t env;
    locker_uuid_t operation_id;
    locker_query_result_t result;
    locker_command_t command;
    locker_status_t status;
    unsigned executes;

    if (!it_bring_up(&env, "borrow")) {
        return;
    }

    TEST_CHECK_STATUS(LOCKER_STATUS_OK, it_create_operation(&env, &operation_id));
    TEST_CHECK(it_wait_operation(&env, &operation_id, "REQUESTED", NULL, IT_WAIT_MS));

    /* 到柜证明：Core 在 presence 里直接推进到 ACCESS_PENDING 并发 EXECUTE。 */
    TEST_CHECK_STATUS(LOCKER_STATUS_OK, it_presence(&env, &operation_id));
    TEST_CHECK(it_wait_operation(&env, &operation_id, "ACCESS_PENDING", "ARMED", IT_WAIT_MS));

    executes = it_counter(&env.server, &env.server.execute_requests);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(1u, executes);
    TEST_CHECK_U64(1u, it_sim_pulses(&env.server));

    /* 物理事实：开门 → 取物 → 关门。物品变化 + 门已关才进入本人确认。 */
    it_set_door(&env.server, LOCKER_WIRE_DOOR_STATE_OPEN);
    it_sleep_ms(120);
    it_set_item(&env.server, LOCKER_WIRE_ITEM_STATE_ABSENT);
    it_sleep_ms(120);
    it_set_door(&env.server, LOCKER_WIRE_DOOR_STATE_CLOSED);
    TEST_CHECK(it_wait_operation(&env, &operation_id, "WAIT_CONFIRM", NULL, IT_WAIT_MS));

    /* 本人确认：revision 必须匹配当前操作版本。 */
    memset(&command, 0, sizeof(command));
    command.principal = it_principal(LOCKER_ROLE_USER);
    command.request_id = it_uuid(0x51u);
    command.resource_id = operation_id;
    command.expected_revision = it_operation_revision(&env, &operation_id);
    snprintf(command.kind, sizeof(command.kind), "%s", LOCKER_CORE_OPERATION_CONFIRM);
    snprintf(command.arguments_json, sizeof(command.arguments_json),
             "{\"operation_revision\":\"%llu\"}", (unsigned long long)command.expected_revision);
    memset(&result, 0, sizeof(result));
    status = it_command(&env, &command, &result);
    TEST_CHECK_STATUS(LOCKER_STATUS_OK, status);
    TEST_CHECK(it_wait_operation(&env, &operation_id, "COMPLETED", "CONFIRMED", IT_WAIT_MS));

    /* 借出完成后必须留下未归还的借用记录，且只有一次 EXECUTE（确认不会开第二次门）。 */
    TEST_CHECK_U64(1u, it_counter(&env.server, &env.server.execute_requests));
    if (it_query(&env, LOCKER_CORE_LOANS_LIST, NULL, &result) == LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        TEST_CHECK(it_json_has(&result, "\"closed_at\":null"));
    }

    it_env_stop(&env);
}

/* 中断位置 1：脉冲开始之前就失败（模拟器事务 BEGIN/INTENT/EVENT/COMMIT/RESULT）。
 * 预期：Core 收到非 OK，进核验；不重放 EXECUTE。 */
static void test_interrupt_before_pulse(void)
#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L

#include "domain_time.h"

#include <string.h>

static bool is_leap_year(int year)
{
    return (year % 4 == 0 && year % 100 != 0) || (year % 400 == 0);
}

static int days_in_month(int year, int month)
{
    static const int k_days[12] = {31, 28, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31};
    if (month < 1 || month > 12) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (month == 2 && is_leap_year(year)) {
        return 29;
    }
    return k_days[month - 1];
}

/* 公历日期 -> 1970-01-01 起的天数（Howard Hinnant 的 days_from_civil）。 */
static int64_t days_from_civil(int year, unsigned month, unsigned day)

    uint32_t slot = UINT32_MAX;

        }
    }

    return slot;

void domain_completion_release(locker_core_t *core, uint32_t slot)
{
    if (core == NULL || slot >= LOCKER_DOMAIN_COMPLETION_SLOTS) {
        return;
    }
    pthread_mutex_lock(&core->completion_mutex);
    domain_completion_t *completion = &core->completions[slot];
    free(completion->json);
    memset(completion, 0, sizeof(*completion));
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&core->completion_mutex);
}

void domain_completion_accept(locker_core_t *core, uint32_t slot, locker_status_t status,
                              const locker_uuid_t *operation_id, bool has_operation)
{
    if (core == NULL || slot >= LOCKER_DOMAIN_COMPLETION_SLOTS) {
        return;
    }
    pthread_mutex_lock(&core->completion_mutex);
    domain_completion_t *completion = &core->completions[slot];
    if (completion->used) {
        completion->accept_status = status;
        completion->has_operation = has_operation;
        if (has_operation && operation_id != NULL) {
            completion->operation_id = *operation_id;
        }
        completion->accept_ready = true;
        pthread_cond_broadcast(&core->completion_cond);
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&core->completion_mutex);
}

void domain_completion_finish(locker_core_t *core, uint32_t slot, locker_status_t status,
                              const char *json, size_t length, uint64_t revision)
{
    if (core == NULL || slot >= LOCKER_DOMAIN_COMPLETION_SLOTS) {
        return;
    }
    char *copy = NULL;
    if (json != NULL && length > 0u) {
        copy = (char *)malloc(length + 1u);
        if (copy == NULL) {
            status = LOCKER_STATUS_NO_SPACE;
        } else {
            memcpy(copy, json, length);
            copy[length] = '\0';
        }
    }
    pthread_mutex_lock(&core->completion_mutex);
    domain_completion_t *completion = &core->completions[slot];
    if (completion->used) {
        free(completion->json);
        completion->json = copy;
        completion->length = (copy != NULL) ? length : 0u;
        completion->final_status = status;
        completion->revision = revision;
        completion->final_ready = true;
        /* 终态保留到保留期结束：调用方可能因为排队延迟而晚到，不能提前清槽。 */
        completion->expires_ms = domain_now(core) + core->completion_retention_ms;
        pthread_cond_broadcast(&core->completion_cond);
    } else {
        free(copy);
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&core->completion_mutex);
}

void domain_completion_fail(locker_core_t *core, uint32_t slot, locker_status_t status)
{
    /* 失败收尾不产生结果体：json 置空、length 置 0，调用方拿到的是纯状态。
       槽已释放（例如结果已被 take 走或已过保留期）时什么也不做，绝不复活旧槽。 */
    if (core == NULL || slot >= LOCKER_DOMAIN_COMPLETION_SLOTS) {
        return;
    }
    pthread_mutex_lock(&core->completion_mutex);
    domain_completion_t *completion = &core->completions[slot];
    if (completion->used) {
        free(completion->json);
        completion->json = NULL;
        completion->length = 0u;
        completion->final_status = status;
        completion->final_ready = true;
        completion->expires_ms = domain_now(core) + core->completion_retention_ms;
        pthread_cond_broadcast(&core->completion_cond);
bool domain_resolution_from_name(const char *text, size_t length, uint8_t *out_resolution)
{
    if (text == NULL || out_resolution == NULL) {
        return false;
    }
    if (name_matches(text, length, "COMPLETE_BORROW")) {
        *out_resolution = LOCKER_RESOLUTION_COMPLETE_BORROW;
        return true;
    }
    if (name_matches(text, length, "COMPLETE_RETURN")) {
        *out_resolution = LOCKER_RESOLUTION_COMPLETE_RETURN;
        return true;
    }
    if (name_matches(text, length, "NO_CHANGE")) {
        *out_resolution = LOCKER_RESOLUTION_NO_CHANGE;
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

bool domain_terminal_state(uint8_t state)
{
    return state == LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_COMPLETED || state == LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_CANCELLED ||
           state == LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_NO_CHANGE || state == LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_FAILED;
}

/* 状态优先级：物理事实只能把操作推到「更强的已发生事实」，不能被更弱的事实回退。
   例如已经见过 PULSE_STARTED（ACCESS_ACTIVE）之后迟到的 COMMAND_ACCEPTED 不能把
   状态拉回 ACCESS_PENDING。数值越大越强。 */
bool domain_state_rank(uint8_t state, uint8_t *out_rank)
{
    if (out_rank == NULL) {
        return false;
    }
    switch (state) {
    case LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REQUESTED:
        *out_rank = 1u;
        return true;
    case LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_PENDING:
        *out_rank = 2u;
        return true;
    case LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_ACTIVE:
        *out_rank = 3u;
        return true;
    case LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_WAIT_CONFIRM:
        *out_rank = 4u;
        return true;
    case LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REVIEW_REQUIRED:
        *out_rank = 5u;
        return true;
    case LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_COMPLETED:
    case LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_CANCELLED:
    case LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_NO_CHANGE:
    case LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_FAILED:
        *out_rank = 6u;
        return true;
    default:
        return false;
    }
}

uint8_t domain_state_from_wire_rank(uint8_t rank)
{
    switch (rank) {
    case 1u:
        return LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REQUESTED;
    case 2u:
        return LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_PENDING;
    case 3u:
        return LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_ACCESS_ACTIVE;
    case 4u:
        return LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_WAIT_CONFIRM;
    case 5u:
        return LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REVIEW_REQUIRED;
    default:
        return LOCKER_OPERATION_STATE_REVIEW_REQUIRED;
    }
}

uint64_t domain_now(locker_core_t *core)
{
    return core->now_ms;
}

uint64_t domain_now_advance(locker_core_t *core, uint64_t candidate)
{
    if (candidate > core->now_ms) {
        core->now_ms = candidate;
    }
    return core->now_ms;
/* ---------- 提交与结果 ---------- */

locker_status_t locker_core_submit(locker_core_t *core, const locker_command_t *command, locker_receipt_t *receipt)
{
    if (core == NULL || command == NULL || receipt == NULL) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    memset(receipt, 0, sizeof(*receipt));
    if (!core->thread_running) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    locker_uuid_t token;
    if (locker_clock_port_new_uuid(&core->clock, &token) != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_IO_ERROR;
    }
    uint32_t slot = domain_completion_acquire(core, &token);
    if (slot == UINT32_MAX) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_BUSY;
    }
    domain_job_t job;
    memset(&job, 0, sizeof(job));
    job.kind = DOMAIN_JOB_COMMAND;
    job.completion = slot;
    job.body.command = *command;
    locker_status_t status = job_enqueue(core, &job);
    if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        domain_completion_release(core, slot);
        return status;
    }
    receipt->submission_token = token;
    receipt->request_id = command->request_id;
    receipt->has_operation = false;
    receipt->status = LOCKER_STATUS_QUEUED;
    return LOCKER_STATUS_QUEUED;
}

locker_status_t locker_core_wait_result(locker_core_t *core, const locker_uuid_t *submission_token,
                                        uint32_t timeout_ms, locker_query_result_t *result)
{
    if (core == NULL || submission_token == NULL || result == NULL) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    memset(result, 0, sizeof(*result));
    uint32_t slot = domain_completion_find(core, submission_token);
    if (slot == UINT32_MAX) {
        /* 未提交过或已过保留期：调用方必须用原幂等键重查（契约 :201）。 */
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    return domain_completion_wait_final(core, slot, timeout_ms, result);
}

## 1.1 生命周期与跨 AI 数据合同

所有函数声明由 `contracts/include/locker_contracts.h` 生成。只有模块 context 保持 opaque；仓储跨模块 DTO 公开为有界 JSON 容器，字段由 `contracts/interna
l-data.schema.json` 固定。

| 接口 | 生命周期或完成规则 |
|---|---|
| `locker_repo_open/query/close` | 数据库路径/schema 与显式首次初始化；query 按集合分页，不暴露 SQLite 连接 |
| `locker_core_create/start/stop/destroy` | create 借用 repo/device/publisher；start 建唯一线程；stop 收敛在途写入，超时禁止 destroy；不关闭借用依
赖 |
| `locker_core_authenticate` | 鉴权经 Core 查会话；命令执行时再次验证，HTTP线程不直接查库 |
| `locker_device_create/open/close/destroy` | create 未打开对象；open建会话；close停止IO/回调不重放动作；线程退出后销毁 |
| `locker_events_create/destroy` | 有界提示发布器；Core停止发布、全部WS关闭后释放 |
| `locker_supervisor_create/destroy` | 固定UI目标与独立限次检查点，不接任意命令 |
| `mcu_control_init/journal_init/health_init` | 返回模块自有静态context；硬件未核对或日志失败仅诊断，不READY、不自动format |

装配顺序：repo_open → device_create → events_create → core_create → device_bind_sink绑定Core接收口 → core_start → device_open → h
ttp_start/ipc_start。停机先http_stop/ipc_stop并等待工作线程结束，再device_close停止回调，core_stop收敛，然后destroy HTTP/IPC/Core/device/publish
er，最后repo_close。未成功停止的线程不得释放对象。构造期由初始化线程独占资源，start后仓储与领域状态交给Core线程；停止后只允许Runtime关闭，不进行新的CRUD。

Runtime每次service启动生成一个非零host_instance_id，传给locker_core_config_t.instance_id与locker_device_config_t.host_instance_id；Cor
e进度/HELLO/HOST_HEALTH/监督SERVICE绑定使用同一实例，不让各模块分别生成。首次真实Core循环前get_progress返回NOT_READY/未知，不能给0计数补一个新采样假装有进展；新进程生成新实例，旧报告不
继承。

`locker_device_bind_sink` 的user、reply/event回调在open前绑定；回调参数只在调用期间借用，接收者复制入Core队列。sink的Core上下文活到device_close等待所有回调结束。回调背压
时不静默丢事件，不ACK未落库数据，暂停新动作和健康授权并要求重新核对，不能无限积压。

`locker_http_start/stop/destroy` 和 `locker_ipc_start/stop/destroy` 是Runtime装配入口。两者借用Core；stop超时不能销毁。Network模块内部WS连接表和发送
helper由同一所有者维护，不另扩跨模块入口。

MCU初始化journal与control后，在启动存储任务前调用 `mcu_journal_bind_sink` 绑定静态Control回执接收者；回执按token复制入Control，绝不在存储回调直接驱动锁。队列满或回执不能交付要锁
存故障、拒绝新动作；没有回执就不能驱动。具体任务创建/ISR通知为阶段2同一固件所有者的本地实现细节。

仓储采用受控文档集合，`locker_change_t` 符合 RepositoryChange；`locker_recovery_t` 符合 RepositoryRecoveryPage。BEGIN IMMEDIATE 内核对 snap
shot_revision 和记录存在/不存在条件，提交文档变化与新版本。slot_guards的key=1保护占位；用户名、UID索引同事务更新。不得向仓储输入SQL，集合与行schema必须匹配。不使用跨模块JSON哈希比较分支，避免
两端序列化差异。

`locker_core_query` 是给HTTP/IPC使用的有界同步包装器：内部排队到Core并以私有submission_token等待最多1000ms；不能在调用线程直接读仓储或可变业务对象，Core线程自身不能调用这个等待包装
器。超时查询可重新发起，没有物理副作用。

幂等request_hash由Core统一生成：用户+方法+规范化路径作为作用域，正文与查询参数按固定ASCII字段名排序、无额外空白、UTF-8保留原字符串、不进行Unicode归一化或浮点转换后SHA-256。Repo只存储/比较Co
re提供的哈希，不自行再次序列化计算。
    job.completion = UINT32_MAX;
    job.body.tick_ms = monotonic_ms;
    return job_enqueue(core, &job);
}

locker_status_t locker_core_query(locker_core_t *core, const locker_query_t *query, locker_query_result_t *result)
{
    if (core == NULL || query == NULL || result == NULL) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_INVALID;
    }
    memset(result, 0, sizeof(*result));
    if (!core->thread_running) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_NOT_READY;
    }
    locker_uuid_t token;
    if (locker_clock_port_new_uuid(&core->clock, &token) != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_IO_ERROR;
    }
    uint32_t slot = domain_completion_acquire(core, &token);
    if (slot == UINT32_MAX) {
        return LOCKER_STATUS_BUSY;
    }
    domain_job_t job;
    memset(&job, 0, sizeof(job));
    job.kind = DOMAIN_JOB_QUERY;
    job.completion = slot;
    job.body.query = *query;
    locker_status_t status = job_enqueue(core, &job);
    if (status != LOCKER_STATUS_OK) {
        domain_completion_release(core, slot);
        return status;
    }
    status = domain_completion_wait_final(core, slot, LOCKER_CORE_RESULT_WAIT_MS, result);
    domain_completion_release(core, slot);
    return status;
}

## 本轮阻塞项

### R3-B-1 / P1 / 实际 EXECUTE 丢弃已冻结的 MCU 期限

- **位置：** `host/domain/src/domain_commands.c:1163,1167,1281`；`host/domain/src/domain_device.c:845,851`。
- **触发与复现：** 给有效锚点偏移 +500000ms、host_now=1000，冻结 outbox_not_after_ms=503000，调用 domain_execute_intent。VM 探针实测 stored=5030
00，sent=2000。
- **影响：** 新锚点只影响持久意图和泵前检查，线上期限仍处于主机时钟域。独立双时钟会误拒或错误放宽期限；同源时钟测试掩盖它。实际发送还用当前 session/physical 重建 guard，没有完整透传冻结意图。依据 inter
nal-interfaces.md:125,133。
- **修复建议：** 发送与已提交 outbox 同一组不可变 guard/期限，校验冻结 session；锚点缺失/过期/跨 boot 拒发。补正负偏移、传输延迟、样本更新/设备重启后实际 intent 字段断言。**阻塞。**

### R3-B-2 / P1 / 上下文释放没有形成下一次动作的屏障

- **位置：** `host/domain/src/domain_device.c:409,418`；`host/domain/src/domain_commands.c:581,1115,1306,1341`；`host/domain
/src/domain_core.c:144,485,487`。
- **触发与复现：** ACCESS_ACTIVE 开关门且物品未变，操作带真实 command_id，事件推进 NO_CHANGE 后泵释放。VM 实测 state=NO_CHANGE、used=0、release_pending=0
、release_calls=0。另在终结落库后、释放完成前提交下一次 presence，武装/发送没有 release_pending 门；core_tick 还先 outbox 后 release。终结提交后进程崩溃也丢失纯内存释放登
记，恢复跳过终态。
- **影响：** MCU has_active 未清，后续 EXECUTE 碰 BUSY；“只开门不取物→再申请”和借还连续使用仍不成立。CONFLICT 也不能证明 MCU 不持有上下文；`:1341` 直接清标志违背 uart-v1
.md:411 的 CAS 失败先查询规则。
- **修复建议：** 统一所有已执行终态的释放入口；以设备查询/匹配回执闭合释放并阻止新武装；重启从持久结果及设备活动上下文恢复核对。补同会话借→还→再借、NO_CHANGE→再申请、释放失败/丢回执/终结后重启的真实 SQLite+模
拟器测试。**阻塞。**

### R3-B-3 / P1 / 新快照中的物理冲突绕过 WAIT_CONFIRM 检查

- **位置：** `host/domain/src/domain_device.c:354,508,579,770`；`host/domain/src/domain_commands.c:783`。
- **触发与复现：** WAIT_CONFIRM 冻结 physical_revision=7，收到同代 CLOSED/ABSENT 但 revision=8 的成功 GET_SNAPSHOT。VM 实测 physical=8，basi
s=7，state 仍 WAIT_CONFIRM；apply_snapshot 没有调用 wait_confirm_conflicted。confirm 重新加载记录后也不检查冻结版本/boot。
- **影响：** 经新快照已知的“开门又关回原状”等冲突可被忽略，仍按旧证据确认，违反 internal-interfaces.md:116。注释称快照同规则并非实现事实。
- **修复建议：** 事件和快照统一执行 boot/epoch/physical_revision 冲突判定；confirm 在同一决策点核对冻结依据，持久化失败也不得继续准许确认。补只经快照得知冲突、UNKNOWN 后恢复和同值新版本
用例。**阻塞。**

### R3-B-4 / P1 / 重启后的 WAIT_CONFIRM 可直接完成

- **位置：** `host/domain/src/domain_core.c:172,447`；`host/domain/src/domain_commands.c:778,783`；`tests/unit/domain/test_d
omain_core.c:538,554`。
- **触发与复现：** 持久化 WAIT_CONFIRM 且 item_transition_seen=true，清 active 后 domain_recover，再以原 revision 确认。VM 探针实测恢复 state=WAI
T_CONFIRM、basis_valid=0，confirm=OK、loan_open=1。恢复仅把 ACCESS_PENDING/ACCESS_ACTIVE 转核验；正式测试反而断言恢复 WAIT_CONFIRM。
- **影响：** 可能已执行的操作在服务重启后绕过核验；单纯换算 120s 不能恢复已丢失的确认上下文。违反 stage-00-architecture.md:119 的可能已执行状态重启规则。
- **修复建议：** WAIT_CONFIRM 重启走持久核验屏障，确认依据缺失时拒绝普通确认；修正目前锁定错误预期的测试。补有/无新事件、期限内/外以及核验落库失败重启用例。**阻塞。**

### R3-B-5 / P1 / 幂等记录仍与业务结果分开提交

- **位置：** `host/domain/src/domain_commands.c:393,419,798,812,847,860,987,1007`。
- **触发与复现：** 只令包含 idempotency mutation 的提交返回 IO_ERROR，允许取消业务事务成功，然后重试原键。VM 探针实测 first=OK、replay=CONFLICT，仓储为 CANCELLED。
confirm/resolve 同样在业务完成后另存记录；进程在两次提交间退出也产生相同窗口。
- **影响：** 已提交结果在丢响应后无法可靠取回，违反 internal-interfaces.md:98 的 operation/loan/审计/幂等同事务要求。revision+1 只使成功路径一致，不能解决原子性；store 失
败还被当成功返回。
- **修复建议：** 在同一 change 预构造最终资源与真实目标 revision，将幂等响应和业务提交一次落库；同键重试返回原结果。补第二步故障/重启场景、响应逐字节及 revision 一致性，不只测连续成功。**阻塞。**

### R3-B-6 / P1 / 人工理由审计失败仍可成功覆盖账本

- **位置：** `host/domain/src/domain_commands.c:824,847,987,998,1036,1057`。
- **触发与复现：** 仅 audits 提交返回 IO_ERROR，执行合法 COMPLETE_BORROW。VM 实测 status=OK、stored_state=COMPLETED、loan_open=1、audits=0。原取
消 reason 仍只解析、不持久化。
- **影响：** 业务已生效却没有实际管理员/理由记录；当前 `(void)` 也没有持久登记缺陷。256 字节理由截断后拼 suffix 还可能切断 UTF-8 字符；不能把“检查过原因”视为“保留原因”。依据 internal-in
terfaces.md:98、contract-source.json 的 resolve 说明。
- **修复建议：** 将真实 actor、结论、完整合法理由及关联信息放进同一终结事务，失败整体不生效；取消原因同样保留。尊重 schema 字符/UTF-8 边界；补失败、重启查询和长中文理由正式回归。**阻塞。**

### R3-B-7 / P1 / 设备状态推进仍先改内存再吞提交错误

- **位置：** `host/domain/src/domain_device.c:477,480,651,666,682,695,740,751`；`host/domain/src/domain_core.c:449,451`。
- **触发与复现：** 库里 ACCESS_PENDING，对 EXECUTE 的 BAD_PRECONDITION 回复注入 commit_status=IO_ERROR。VM 实测 active=REVIEW_REQUIRED、st
ored=ACCESS_PENDING、publish_calls=1。PULSE_STARTED、QUERY 回复和恢复转核验同样直接改 active，再忽略持久化结果。
- **影响：** helper 的 candidate 修复没有覆盖所有调用链；内存/数据库/发布版本再次分叉。内存已是核验时 domain_require_review 短路 OK，不能自动补库；恢复还可在失败后设置 recovery
_ready。
- **修复建议：** 所有响应/事件/恢复跃迁统一提交候选，再安装及发布；提交失败保留可重试或显式故障闭锁状态。补上述各入口 IO_ERROR→恢复→再核对的数据库/内存/发布联合断言。**阻塞。**

### R3-B-8 / P1 / 丢 EXECUTE 应答后的等待仍可能永久锁住

- **位置：** `host/domain/src/domain_commands.c:1374,1391,1417,1446`；`host/domain/src/domain_device.c:715,733`。
- **触发与复现：** EXECUTE 应答丢失，但门/物品事件在 5s 内已推进 WAIT_CONFIRM。VM 探针推进到 7000ms 后 query_calls=0、execute_in_flight=1、pending=1。查
询仅在 ACCESS_PENDING/ACCESS_ACTIVE 发起；若 QUERY 自身也丢失，超时清 pending 仍要求 !execute_in_flight。操作终结后 apply_query_command 又因 !used
 提前返回。
- **影响：** 后续 outbox 永久撞在飞门；仅证明“ACK 丢失时发生了一次查询、不重复脉冲”不能证明下一次业务可用。
- **修复建议：** 命令等待生命周期独立于业务状态，所有可能已执行状态均有有界查询/核验收敛；结果未知只阻断旧命令重发，不无限占用新命令槽。补快速到 WAIT_CONFIRM、查询丢失/迟到、完成/核验后再次操作。**阻塞。**

### R3-B-9 / P1 / 显式 tool_revision=0 可绕过版本检查

- **位置：** `host/domain/src/domain_commands.c:574`；`contracts/contract-source.json:726`。
- **触发与复现：** 当前工具 revision=1，提交必填 tool_revision 文本“0”。VM 探针 result=OK。代码给 0 加了“兼容旧客户端”的未授权例外。
- **影响：** 不匹配版本仍可占柜并进入动作链；真源要求提交版本并做乐观检查，没有“不声明版本”的分支。
- **修复建议：** 精确匹配当前版本，不私增兼容语义；补 0/陈旧/未来版本及真实仓储恢复后的工具内容测试。**阻塞。**

### R3-B-10 / P2 / 跨模块阻塞 IO 仍违反本卡禁止项

- **位置：** `host/domain/src/domain_device.c:185,211,250,854`；`host/domain/src/domain_commands.c:1330`；`host/device/src/p
ort.c:523`；`host/device/src/transport.c:192,222`。
- **触发与复现：** AF_UNIX 对端保持连接但不读，缓冲耗尽，Core 发送调用进入 write_lock 与最长 1000ms 的 poll。当前调用链仍存在；本轮未新增背压运行测试。
- **影响：** 单所有者循环的超时、队列和进度同步停顿，违反 module-ownership.md:23 及 S1-04 “不调用阻塞网络”。CR-S104-2 说明所有权限制，不构成验收豁免，也不能改列已修缺陷。
- **修复建议：** 由设备协议所有者/统一集成负责人实现有界非阻塞发送队列并交付，Core 消费异步结果；增加持续背压下的进度与等待上限回归后再复审。**阻塞。**

### R3-B-11 / P1 / UNKNOWN 和人工 NO_CHANGE 被误当原基线

- **位置：** `host/domain/src/domain_device.c:389,390,409`；`host/domain/src/domain_commands.c:939,964,976`；`docs/stages/st
age-00-architecture.md:128`。
- **触发与复现：** ACCESS_ACTIVE 已见开门，关门事件 item=UNKNOWN，VM 实测直接 NO_CHANGE、used=0。另 BORROW 核验时当前 ABSENT、原基线 PRESENT，管理员选择 NO_C
HANGE，VM 实测返回 OK、落 NO_CHANGE。
- **影响：** “不是目标值”不等于“始终未变化且回到原基线”；未知/物品已离位却释放占位、账本保持不变，造成物理与账本不一致。
- **修复建议：** 明确区分目标、原基线、UNKNOWN/矛盾三类；未知或证据不完整进核验；管理员 NO_CHANGE 也校验方向对应原基线。补借/还两向 UNKNOWN、变位后恢复、人工反向物品状态测试。**阻塞。**

### R3-B-12 / P1 / 实时事件跳号仍能声称完整并进入确认

- **位置：** `host/domain/src/domain_device.c:438,442,447,451,494,503`；`host/device/src/port.c:354,386`。
- **触发与复现：** 同 epoch/boot 输入实时 seq=1 开门、seq=3 离位、seq=4 关门，跳过 seq=2。VM 探针实测 continuity=1、fetch_gap=0、history_complete=1、
state=WAIT_CONFIRM。DevicePort 只校验会话/解码，不替 Core 检查事件序号连续。
- **影响：** 缺失的事件可能含物理冲突，Core 却提前宣称完整证据；后续 FETCH 尚未补齐期间可确认。当前 domain_import_page 的连续性派生不能追认未提交的实时水位。
- **修复建议：** 检测相邻键/跨 boot 屏障，在证据推进前锁存已知缺口并阻止确认；以落库连续水位证明完整性。补跳号、延迟补页、缺口页、跨 boot 和补齐前并发确认回归。**阻塞。**

### R3-B-13 / P1 / 取消失败仍把 UTC 截止点安装进 active

- **位置：** `host/domain/src/domain_state.c:493,495,497`；`host/domain/src/domain_commands.c:831,846,847,1356`。
- **触发与复现：** REQUESTED 的正确单调截止为 121000ms，取消前从库加载操作，终结提交注入 IO_ERROR。VM 实测 status=IO_ERROR、mono=1000、active.deadline=1761
004920000；恢复仓储并推进 130s 后仍 REQUESTED、used=1。
- **影响：** 只修 recover_operations 没有修普通加载；cancel 在调用 candidate helper 前已覆盖 active，失败破坏原超时状态，占位不能正常退出。与 R3-B-7 的设备响应分支是独立触
发路径。
- **修复建议：** 保持单调截止的统一恢复/加载规则，在整个命令成功前不覆盖活动工作副本；确认/核验附加字段也在局部副本准备。补取消失败→超时、墙钟未知/过期/回拨和反复重试。**阻塞。**

### R3-B-14 / P1 / completion 槽复用可交付另一请求结果

- **位置：** `host/domain/src/domain_core.c:791,796,874,875`；`host/domain/src/domain_repo.c:364,400,447,460`。
- **触发与复现：** wait_result 分别加锁执行 find(token) 和 wait_final(slot)，两者间旧终态到期被 reap、新请求复用槽。VM 确定性交错探针实测 found_a=0、slot_b=0、to
ken_equal=0，但等待旧槽返回 OK / OTHER_USER_B。另 locker_core_query 超时后直接 release，尚在队列/处理中的旧 job 仍只保存槽号，可完成已经复用的新槽。
- **影响：** 锁保护了单步内存访问，却没有保护请求身份/槽生命周期，可能把他人的查询或业务结果交给旧调用者；TSan 全绿不会检测这种逻辑竞态。
- **修复建议：** 完成和等待均绑定不可复用 token/代号，锁内重新校验身份；查询超时不能让仍在途 job 写入可重用裸槽。补排队超过 1s、终态过期边界与两个不同用户并发复用的确定性回归。**阻塞。**

andoff “S1-05 可立即开始”均不符本轮反例。集成 test_borrow_flow 到借出即结束，没有真实连续归还；域源码当前合计 9346 行，集成文件 1553 行，旧行数不再准确。handoff 未记录整卡实际耗时及审计
轮数，并依赖 /tmp 临时脚本作复核入口。

### R3-N-3 / P2 / 独立审计归档尚未满足逐轮四件套

### R3-N-4 / P2 / 设备 mode 仍由 Core phase 冒充

- **事实与影响：** Core 为 READY 就公开设备 READY，即使当前 session 不可用、快照失败或实际设备处于 RECONCILING。核验新增加的 freshness 门已阻止原“旧缓存改账”反例，但显示仍可能误导
调用端；两者不能合称全部修复。
- **建议：** 从真实会话/设备模式/快照健康派生设备投影，测试失联与重连阶段；保留未知，不重置旧样本年龄。**非阻塞投影项。**
